# EFFECTS-CATALOG - every effect the engine performs, one card each

Generated by `python content/video_engine/scripts/build_effects_catalog.py --write` from `content/video_engine/effects/cards/*.json` (schema `content/video_engine/configs/effect_card.schema.json`). Do not hand-edit it:
`--check` fails when it drifts from the cards, the compiler's `SPECIES_WHEN` / `CHART_TO_WHEN` (pulled into
`when` on the species, page_species and chart_to axes), the module dial objects or `docs/DOCS-INDEX.jsonl`.

The recipe - one call:

```bash
python content/video_engine/scripts/effects_card.py "<name>"
rg -i "<name>" docs/EFFECTS-CATALOG.md
```

Each card is: title - id - does - when - the example - phases as a numbered list - blends - options -
lives (module | inline | compiler-only | declared-unbuilt, the path and the one symbol) - dials read from the
module - status and callable - proof - doctrine cites resolved to path:line - aliases. A token that is only a
parameter of an effect is an option on its card, never a card.

143 cards, 92 options, 18 axes. 45 recipes (15 proven).

## species

### The numbered agenda species

- **id** `species:agenda` - **does** Two to four numbered rows are revealed one per word and hold at a named idle; form "page" gives the same list a plate of its own, each row stamped with its catalogued icon once its sentence has been read.
- **when** the sentence SETS an agenda ('two numbers', 'three things') - 2 to 4 numbered rows, each revealed on its own word, holding at a named idle until the sentence that takes them one by one (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "agenda", "at": 5.0, "dur": 14.0, "idle": "breath", "rows": [{"text": "A Treasury page", "sub": "the sellers"}, {"text": "Your phone", "at": 6.2, "sub": "the bill"}], "target": {"kind": "region", "x0": 0.30, "y0": 0.50, "x1": 0.95, "y1": 0.95}}` (content/video_engine/tests/golden/build_golden_sources.py:1030; key: species[] {"kind": "agenda", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **number writes** - the row's numeral fades in (trigger: the row's own `at` (else STEP after the previous row); dials: `STEP`=0.34, `ROW_S`=0.42)
  2. **rule draws** - the nib draws the hairline under the row (trigger: the row's `at`; dials: `RULE_S`=0.5)
  3. **text rises** - the row's text rises ROW_DY and fades in (trigger: NUM_LEAD after the row's `at`; dials: `NUM_LEAD`=0.12, `ROW_DY`=26)
  4. **hold alive** - each row holds on its own phase of the idle (breath by default) (trigger: continuous)
  5. **icon stamps (page form)** - the row's catalogued cutout falls onto the page and squashes on impact, once that row's sentence has been read (E93) (trigger: PAGE_STAMP_LAG after the row's own `at` + NUM_LEAD + ROW_S; dials: `PAGE_STAMP_LAG`=0.12, `PAGE_STAMP_S`=0.26, `PAGE_SQUASH`=0.14)
- **blend** Bravos 'China's Gameplan 1 | 2' board -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/agenda.mjs:3)
- **blend** E93 an agenda row carries an icon, stamped on once its sentence has been read -> the page form's stamp (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2761)
- **options** `form` `form: "page"` is the agenda's ONE named form - the plate version of the list (E99 s16). Absent is the dock form, byte-identical to what it always was; any other word is refused by name.; `page` form=page: the rows divide the whole board, each on a mount with its numeral in a medallion, under a title, and each carries a catalogued icon (E93/E94) stamped on after its sentence.
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/agenda.mjs` - symbol `paintAgenda`
- **dials** `AGENDA` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/agenda.mjs`: `MIN_ROWS`=2, `MAX_ROWS`=4, `ROW_H`=132, `NUM_W`=92, `NUM_SIZE`=64, `TEXT_SIZE`=54, `SUB_SIZE`=34, `ROW_S`=0.42, `ROW_DY`=26, `RULE_S`=0.5, `RULE_DY`=22, `NUM_LEAD`=0.12, `STEP`=0.34, `MIN_K`=0.45, `PAGE_FORM`="page", `PROP_KEY`="prop:", `PAGE_PAD`=48, `PAGE_TITLE_H`=158, `PAGE_TITLE_SIZE`=62, `PAGE_TITLE_K`=1.15, `PAGE_MAX_K`=1.8, `PAGE_ROW_PAD`=28, `PAGE_MED`=0.30, `PAGE_MED_W`=3.5, `PAGE_ICON`=0.70, `PAGE_ICON_GAP`=44, `PAGE_TEXT_Y`=0.46, `PAGE_SUB_Y`=0.79, `PAGE_PLATE_A`=0.30, `PAGE_PLATE_R`=14, `PAGE_STAMP_LAG`=0.12, `PAGE_STAMP_S`=0.26, `PAGE_STAMP_FROM`=1.45, `PAGE_SETTLE_S`=0.14, `PAGE_SQUASH`=0.14, `PAGE_FADE_S`=0.1, `PAGE_INK`="#05131e", `PAGE_EDGE`="#8a94a0", `PAGE_GOLD`="#F5B72E", `PAGE_CHALK`="#F2F2F2", `PAGE_HALO`="rgba(27,30,35,.85)", `PAGE_FACE`="Inter, Arial, sans-serif"
- **status** wired - **callable** yes
- **proof** golden agenda-two - test content/video_engine/tests/kinetics/agenda.test.mjs - first use none
- **doctrine** CAPABILITIES NUMBERED AGENDA -> docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:43; E93 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2761
- **aliases** "THE NUMBERED AGENDA" (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:37)

### The vector map arc species

- **id** `species:arc` - **does** A clothoid arc draws from one place to another on the word, struck with an X when the flow is cut.
- **when** the sentence NAMES a flow between two places - the arc draws from one to the other on the word; crossed when the flow is cut (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "arc", "at": 6.6, "dur": 12.0, "crossed": 11.5, "from": {"kind": "mappoint", "x": 644, "y": 178}, "to": {"kind": "country", "id": "USA"}}` (content/video_engine/tests/golden/build_golden_sources.py:586; key: species[] {"kind": "arc", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **shaft draws** - the clothoid draws by length from one place to the other (trigger: the species' `at`; dials: `DRAW_S`=0.9)
  2. **head lands** - the arrowhead draws over the last share of the clock (trigger: HEAD_F of DRAW_S; dials: `HEAD_F`=0.74)
  3. **cut** - an X is struck at the midpoint and the arc dims (the X does not) (trigger: `crossed`; dials: `CROSS_S`=0.45, `DIM`=0.42)
- **blend** clothoid (42 s42.4) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/42-DRAWING-KINETICS.md:102)
- **blend** the chip's X strokes (chipStrokes) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:87)
- **blend** Bravos world map, shots 57-80 -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:92)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/vecmap.mjs` - symbol `paintArc` - from = VECMAP_GULF written out (golden :565); plate must be vecmap
- **dials** `ARC` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/vecmap.mjs`: `DRAW_S`=0.9, `BOW`=0.5, `ENTER_K`=0.45, `SAMPLES`=48, `HEAD`=26, `HEAD_A`=0.42, `HEAD_F`=0.74, `CROSS_S`=0.45, `CROSS_ARM`=26, `DIM`=0.42
- **status** wired - **callable** yes
- **proof** golden vecmap-arc - test content/video_engine/tests/kinetics/vecmap.test.mjs - first use none
- **doctrine** CAPABILITIES VECTOR MAP -> docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:99

- **when** leaving a chart as a HIT - the final state freezes, then a directional cut (declared in 29 s9.27, NOT built) (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'beat_freeze', 'at': 12.0, 'dur': 1.6, 'target': {'kind': 'point', 'x': 0.5, 'y': 0.5}}` (authored; key: species[] {"kind": "beat_freeze", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)

- **status** declared - **callable** no: compiles with no painter (SPECIES_WHEN: not built; 0 engine hits, no species module)

### The scribble callout species

- **id** `species:callout` - **does** A hand-drawn ring closes round a number or a point on a chart, with its label popping beside it.
- **when** the sentence names a NUMBER or a POINT on a chart to ring (E56: a ring's one use); the label is the sentence's figure (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'callout', 'at': 9.77, 'dur': 7.65, 'label': '25%', 'pad': 22, 'label_scale': 2.2, 'target': {'kind': 'point', 'x': 0.65, 'y': 0.34}}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/japan-tariff-trick/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:5; key: species[] {"kind": "callout", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **circle draws** - the hand-drawn ellipse draws on by dash (trigger: the species' `at`; dials: `CALLOUT.DRAW_S`=0.7)
  2. **label pops** - the label scales 0.7 -> 1.0 on spEase over 0.25 s (trigger: CALLOUT.LABEL_AT (0.8) x DRAW_S into the draw; dials: `CALLOUT.DRAW_S`=0.7)
  3. **underline form (alternative)** - with form: underline, a squiggle stroke draws under the press card's phrase instead (trigger: the species' `at`; dials: `SQUIG_DRAW`=0.45)
- **blend** hw-callout-circle (doc 29 harvest) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1800)
- **blend** E56 a ring's one use -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1815)
- **blend** squiggle law for the underline form -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1805)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/callout.mjs` - symbol `paintCallout` - also `calloutPath` - promoted from the engine's own branch (P57 T17 / R26-97): the module registers SPECIES_PAINTERS.callout and is inlined into the engine between KINETICS:BEGIN callout and KINETICS:END
- **status** live - **callable** yes
- **proof** golden chart-callout - test content/video_engine/tests/test_targeted_species.py::test_callout_without_a_target_is_an_error_naming_the_kind - first use japan-tariff-trick build-short (APPROVED 2026-09-09, REVIEW-CLAUDE.md:5; table read today, timeline rebuilt 2026-09-10T10:01 after the render 2026-09-09T07:44) t=9.77
- **doctrine** 29 s9.27 -> docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1783; 29 s9.27 row Scribble callout -> docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1783; E56 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1815
- **aliases** "Scribble callout" (docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1800); "A ring has ONE use" (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1815)

### The icon chip species

- **id** `species:chip` - **does** A card with one sourced glyph and a label lands on its word and is crossed out on a later word.
- **when** the sentence names a THING as one of a set (a prediction, an actor, a plant) - a chip lands on its word; RETRACTS crosses it out on a later word (Bravos's icon board) (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "chip", "at": 10.0, "dur": 6.0, "icon": "factory", "label": "STEEL", "target": {"kind": "point", "x": 0.3, "y": 0.5}}` (content/video_engine/tests/test_targeted_species.py:176; key: species[] {"kind": "chip", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **land** - the card springs from POP_FROM and falls DROP_PX onto its word, fading in (trigger: the species' `at`; dials: `LAND_S`=0.55, `POP_FROM`=0.82, `DROP_PX`=34, `FADE_S`=0.14)
  2. **hold alive** - holds on its named idle (trigger: landing complete)
  3. **cross out** - two X strokes draw one after the other and the card dims (trigger: `cross_at`; dials: `CROSS_S`=0.5, `DIM`=0.55)
- **blend** Bravos icon board, shots 26-28 -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:87)
- **blend** the dock's spring (springPop, 42 s42.2) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/42-DRAWING-KINETICS.md:63)
- **blend** the curvature stroke for the X (42 s42.1) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/42-DRAWING-KINETICS.md:12)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/chip.mjs` - symbol `paintChip`
- **dials** `CHIP` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/chip.mjs`: `SIZE`=168, `RX`=22, `GLYPH`=92, `LABEL_DY`=46, `LAND_S`=0.55, `POP_FROM`=0.82, `DROP_PX`=34, `FADE_S`=0.14, `CROSS_S`=0.5, `DIM`=0.55
- **status** wired - **callable** yes
- **proof** golden chip-board - test content/video_engine/tests/kinetics/chip.test.mjs - first use none
- **doctrine** CAPABILITIES icon CHIP -> docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:94; P50 T2 -> unresolved

### The isometric count array species

- **id** `species:count_array` - **does** N identical sourced icons arrive in reading order on a 2:1 rhombus lattice and the count is written under them.
- **when** the sentence COUNTS a set and the count IS the claim ('six plants', 'twelve refineries') - N identical icons arrive in reading order on an isometric field and the number is written under them (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "count_array", "at": 5.0, "dur": 14.0, "count": 6, "icon": "factory", "idle": "breath", "claim": "SIX PLANTS", "target": {"kind": "region", "x0": 0.08, "y0": 0.47, "x1": 0.92, "y1": 0.97}}` (content/video_engine/tests/golden/build_golden_sources.py:1026; key: species[] {"kind": "count_array", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **icons arrive** - one icon per word lands on the chip's spring in reading order on the 2:1 lattice (trigger: at + i x STEP (or the row's `step`); dials: `STEP`=0.34, `LAND_S`=0.45, `POP_FROM`=0.86, `DROP_PX`=26, `FADE_S`=0.12)
  2. **claim writes** - the count pops in under the field (0.72 -> 1.0) (trigger: CLAIM_LAG after the last icon settles; dials: `CLAIM_LAG`=0.18, `CLAIM_S`=0.4)
  3. **hold alive** - every cell holds on its own phase of the idle (breath by default) (trigger: continuous)
- **blend** Bravos count field (exploration review #59) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/EXPLORATION-REVIEW-2026-09-10.md:59)
- **blend** the chip's landing law -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:87)
- **blend** doc 46 word pitch (178 WPM) -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/countarray.mjs:45)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/countarray.mjs` - symbol `paintCountArray`
- **dials** `COUNT` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/countarray.mjs`: `MIN`=2, `MAX`=12, `PITCH`=190, `RISE`=0.5, `ICON`=120, `TILE_K`=0.86, `STEP`=0.34, `LAND_S`=0.45, `POP_FROM`=0.86, `DROP_PX`=26, `FADE_S`=0.12, `CLAIM_LAG`=0.18, `CLAIM_S`=0.4, `CLAIM_DY`=78, `CLAIM_SIZE`=58, `MIN_K`=0.4

### The flow diagram species

- **id** `species:flow` - **does** Two to six named things and the clothoid arrows between them draw on; later one node swaps while the rest stands.
- **when** the sentence EXPLAINS a mechanism - A causes B via C - as named things and the arrows between them; a later word SWAPS one node and the rest stands (Bravos's rhyme) (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "flow", "at": 4.0, "dur": 18.0, "idle": "breath", "target": {"kind": "region", "x0": 0.10, "y0": 0.50, "x1": 0.90, "y1": 0.90}, "nodes": [{"id": "plant", "icon": "factory", "label": "PLANTS"}, {"id": "freight", "icon": "ship", "label": "FREIGHT"}, {"id": "price", "icon": "coins", "label": "PRICE"}], "edges": [["plant", "freight"], ["freight", "price"]], "swap": {"at": 11.0, "node": "freight", "icon": "cpu", "label": "CHIPS"}, "tag": "1973"}` (content/video_engine/tests/golden/build_golden_sources.py:514; key: species[] {"kind": "flow", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **frame draws** - the dashed box draws clockwise from the top-left, dash by dash (trigger: the species' `at`; dials: `BOX_S`=0.9)
  2. **nodes land** - chips land one after another on the chip's spring (trigger: BOX_LEAD of the frame's draw, then every NODE_STEP; dials: `BOX_LEAD`=0.55, `NODE_STEP`=0.2, `LAND_S`=0.55)
  3. **arrows draw** - clothoid arrows draw one at a time by length; each head lands over the last share of its clock (trigger: EDGE_LAG after the last node lands; dials: `EDGE_LAG`=0.1, `EDGE_S`=0.34, `HEAD_F`=0.72)
  4. **year stamps** - the tag springs in at the box's top-right corner (trigger: TAG_LAG after the last arrow; dials: `TAG_LAG`=0.12, `TAG_S`=0.5)
  5. **swap** - the named node's landing runs backward and fades, then the new node lands in its place while the rest stand (trigger: `swap.at`; dials: `SWAP_OUT_S`=0.3, `SWAP_IN_S`=0.45)
- **blend** Bravos flow diagram, shots 82-86 -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:89)
- **blend** clothoid arrows (42 s42.4) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/42-DRAWING-KINETICS.md:102)
- **blend** the chip's landing -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:87)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/flow.mjs` - symbol `paintFlow`
- **dials** `FLOW` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/flow.mjs`: `BOX_S`=0.9, `BOX_LEAD`=0.55, `DASH`=26, `DASH_GAP`=15, `NODE_STEP`=0.2, `EDGE_LAG`=0.1, `EDGE_S`=0.34, `BOW`=0.42, `ENTER_K`=0.45, `EDGE_GAP`=16, `HEAD`=28, `HEAD_A`=0.42, `HEAD_F`=0.72, `SAMPLES`=40, `SWAP_OUT_S`=0.3, `SWAP_IN_S`=0.45, `TAG_LAG`=0.12, `TAG_S`=0.5, `TAG_PAD`=26, `TAG_SIZE`=34, `PITCH_K`=1.5, `CROSS_K`=1.15, `MIN_K`=0.4, `LABEL_H`=34

### The focus zoom species

- **id** `species:focus_zoom` - **does** After a chart or document has entered, the view zooms and pans to one region and holds dead still.
- **when** after a chart or document has entered, the sentence turns to ONE region of it and holds there dead still (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'focus_zoom', 'at': 3.0, 'dur': 2.0, 'target': {'kind': 'datum', 'index': 4}}` (authored; key: species[] {"kind": "focus_zoom", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **zoom + pan** - scales to the anchor on an in-out ease over the first 1/1.8 of the window (trigger: the species' `at`; dials: `FOCUS_SCALE`=1.32)
  2. **hold dead still** - the servo law: no drift once there (trigger: k * 1.8 >= 1)
- **blend** ui-focus-zoom (doc 29 harvest) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1801)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `camXf` - camXf / sp.kind === "focus_zoom"
- **dials** `CAM` in `content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/camera.mjs`: `PUNCH_IN`=0.42, `PUNCH_OUT`=0.5, `PUNCH_SCALE`=1.14, `FOCUS_SCALE`=1.32, `PULL_FROM`=1.9

### The feathered spotlight species

- **id** `species:spotlight` - **does** The frame dims except a feathered light that lands on a picture or datum and holds for the sentence.

  2. **glide** - the hole glides from target to target2 on spIO over 0.6 s (trigger: at + glide_at)

  4. **dim out** - fades out over the last 0.4 s (trigger: 0.4 s before at + dur)

### The steam still-life species

- **id** `species:steam` - **does** Wisps of steam or smoke rise off a named region of an approved still so the plate breathes.
- **when** STILL LIFE: a named region of an approved still breathes (steam, smoke) so the plate never goes still (E49) (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'steam', 'at': 38.96, 'dur': 5.92, 'target': {'kind': 'region', 'x0': 0.8, 'y0': 0.55, 'x1': 0.95, 'y1': 0.6}}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:5; key: species[] {"kind": "steam", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **fade in** - three seeded wisps fade in over 0.6 s (trigger: the species' `at`)
  2. **rise loop** - each wisp rises and curls on its own phase of the period (trigger: continuous; dials: `STEAM_PERIOD`=2.6)
  3. **fade out** - fades out over the last 0.6 s (trigger: 0.6 s before at + dur)

### The trace hops species

- **id** `species:trace` - **does** A route draws between named points on a still, with hops, and stamps collect at each named point.
- **when** the sentence NAMES places and flows on a still - a route draws with hops between named points, stamps stack at them (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'trace', 'at': 9.22, 'dur': 8.2, 'color': '#B0201F', 'target': {'kind': 'region', 'x0': 0.215, 'y0': 0.34, 'x1': 0.65, 'y1': 0.425}, 'hop': {'from': [0.215, 0.425], 'to': [0.65, 0.34], 'bow': 0.16, 'draw_s': 0.55, 'width': 9}}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/japan-tariff-trick/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:5; key: species[] {"kind": "trace", "at", "dur", "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **draw** - a seeded zigzag (or, with `hop`, one quadratic bow) draws on by dash (trigger: the species' `at` (and every TRACE.PERIOD for the plain trace); dials: `TRACE.DRAW_S`=1.1)
  2. **arrowhead lands** - the arrowhead appears when the line completes (trigger: draw fraction reaches 1)
  3. **hold / fade / repeat** - plain: holds, fades over the last 18 % of the period, redraws; hop: held to the end (trigger: phase > 0.82 of TRACE.PERIOD (plain only); dials: `TRACE.PERIOD`=3.2)
- **blend** Trace HOPS + stacked stamps -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:71)

## page_species

### The agenda page

- **id** `page_species:agenda` - **does** The list takes a plate of its own: a title over rows that divide the whole board, each on its mount with its numeral in a medallion, and each row's catalogued icon stamped on once its sentence has been read.
- **when** the sentence SETS an agenda ('two numbers', 'three things') - 2 to 4 numbered rows, each revealed on its own word, holding at a named idle until the sentence that takes them one by one (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "agenda", "form": "page", "at": 5.0, "dur": 9.0, "idle": "breath", "title": "WHAT THE BILL IS MADE OF", "rows": [{"text": "A Treasury page", "icon": "prop-icon-us-sovereign-markets-v1"}, {"text": "The rate they pay", "at": 6.6, "icon": "prop-icon-interest-rates-monetary-policy-v2"}, {"text": "Your bill", "at": 8.2, "sub": "$4,500", "icon": "prop-icon-cpi-inflation-basket-v1"}], "target": {"kind": "region", "x0": 0.05, "y0": 0.08, "x1": 0.95, "y1": 0.94}}` (content/video_engine/tests/golden/build_golden_sources.py:1364; key: species[] {"kind": "agenda", "form": "page", "at", "dur", "title", "rows": [{"text", "icon"}], "target"} (shot row 7th element); check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **the title writes** - the title fades in and the nib draws its rule under it, closing the title's room off the list (trigger: the block's own `at`, over ROW_S; dials: `PAGE_TITLE_H`=158, `ROW_S`=0.42)
  2. **a row is mounted and numbered** - the row's plate fades up, the nib draws its medallion and the numeral is written in it (trigger: the row's own `at` (else STEP after the row before); dials: `PAGE_PLATE_A`=0.30, `PAGE_MED`=0.30)
  3. **the sentence is written** - the row's text rises into place, its sub lands beside it as a figure, and the rule draws along the row's bottom edge (trigger: NUM_LEAD after the row's `at`, over ROW_S; dials: `NUM_LEAD`=0.12, `ROW_DY`=26, `RULE_S`=0.5)
  4. **the icon stamps** - the row's catalogued cutout falls from PAGE_STAMP_FROM and squashes on impact - never before that row's sentence has been read (E93) (trigger: PAGE_STAMP_LAG after the row's sentence is fully written; dials: `PAGE_STAMP_LAG`=0.12, `PAGE_STAMP_S`=0.26, `PAGE_STAMP_FROM`=1.45, `PAGE_SQUASH`=0.14)
  5. **hold alive** - every row holds on its own phase of the named idle (E49) (trigger: continuous)
- **blend** Steel and Paper's three-question TEST card (the operator's pointer, E93) -> the mount, the title and the mark per row (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/checklist.mjs)
- **blend** E94 the operator's 44 woodblock cutouts, approved for render -> the stamped icon (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2778)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/agenda.mjs` - symbol `paintAgendaPage`

### The bracket page species

- **id** `page_species:bracket` - **does** The hand draws a measured span between two data, the number as its label.
- **when** the sentence SPANS two data ('from the peak to June', 'a tenth of the pile') - the hand draws the span, the number is its label, the second thing its sub (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'bracket', 'at': 46.72, 'dur': 2.4, 'from': 311, 'to': 315, 'label': '−$122.6B', 'sub': 'a tenth of the pile', 'color': 'neg'}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:7; key: species[] {"kind": "bracket", "at", "dur", ...} on a ledger row; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **span draws** - the hairline (or bar form) draws between the two data (trigger: the species' `at`, over BRACKET_DRAW of `dur`; dials: `BRACKET_DRAW`=0.5)
  2. **ticks pop** - the end ticks spring open (trigger: after BRACKET_DRAW, over BRACKET_TICK; dials: `BRACKET_TICK`=0.15)
  3. **label writes** - the label writes glyph by glyph (70 %), then the sub (30 %) (trigger: BRACKET_LABEL of `dur`; dials: `BRACKET_LABEL`=0.55)
  4. **retract (optional)** - an undraw after it takes the bracket with the line on the undraw's clock (trigger: a series-less `undraw` or a recast/morph after the bracket)
- **blend** Build-on (P47 T2) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:67)
- **blend** Bravos shot 36 bar form -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs:5623)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `paintBracket`
- **status** wired - **callable** yes

### The retitle page species

- **id** `page_species:retitle` - **does** The page's title rewrites by the hand; a returning page arrives retitled.

  2. **write** - the new title writes glyph by glyph (trigger: ERASE_S after `at`, over dur - ERASE_S)

### The span page species

- **id** `page_species:span` - **does** A shaded stretch of time sits behind the chart with its name above it. The shade is DARKER than the page by default (E99 s46: span_tone dark at span_alpha 0.30); span_tone light is the old chalk wash, by name.
- **when** the sentence SPANS a period on a chart - a regime, an epoch, 'the decade' - shaded behind the line with its name; a bracket measures two data, a span names a stretch of time (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py SPECIES_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "span", "at": 8.0, "dur": 8.0, "from": 40, "to": 150, "label": "THE RUN-UP", "color": "cobalt"}` (content/video_engine/tests/golden/build_golden_sources.py:539; key: species[] {"kind": "span", "at", "dur", ...} on a ledger row; check: species)

  1. **shade arrives** - the band fades in behind the chart, darkening it (trigger: the species' `at`; dials: `IN_S`=0.45, `TONE`=dark (the default, E99 s46), `ALPHA_DARK`=0.30, `ALPHA`=0.16 (the light tone's own law))
  2. **name writes** - the period's name writes glyph by glyph above (or inside) the band (trigger: IN_S after `at`, over WRITE of `dur`; dials: `WRITE`=0.5)

## chart_to

### The compare chart-to verb (metric to comparator)

- **id** `chart_to:compare` - **does** The figure the market quotes becomes the number the viewer feels: the metric's own outlines - measured off the page's ink - sag and BALL UP at its datum, and the ball becomes the comparator's glyphs (or bursts, or is thrown).
- **when** the sentence quotes the market's figure and then says what it MEANS - the quoted number morphs into the number the viewer feels (E76); the arithmetic is authored, never invented (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py CHART_TO_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "chart_to", "at": 32.0, "dur": 1.2, "to": "compare", "metric": {"value": 24.8, "text": "24.8x", "label": "forward P/E"}, "comparator": {"value": 0.1535, "text": "15 % dearer", "label": "dearer than its own history"}, "inputs": {"pe": 24.8, "hist": 21.5}, "derive": "pe / hist - 1", "source": "[DERIVED: from ev-meta-pe-v1 + the 10-year median, pe / hist - 1]"}` (content/video_engine/tests/test_metric_comparator.py:32; key: chart_to {"to": "compare"}; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **the ink sags** - `form: melt` (the default): the figure's own glyph outlines - measured off the page's ink by kinetics/contour.mjs - sag on melt.mjs's law, the top sinking and the foot hanging in seeded drips (trigger: the chart_to's `at`, over MELT.MELT_END of `dur`; dials: `SAG_GAIN`=3.2, `MELT.SAG`=0.40, `MELT.TOP_SAG`=0.42)
  2. **the ink balls up** - every outline is carried by morph_a into ONE disc that holds the ink's own area (never MELT.BALL_R, a share of a PAGE's height), on melt's stepped clock: the number compiles into a heavy ball (trigger: MELT.MELT_END of `dur`, to MELT.BALL_END; dials: `BALL_SWELL`=1.0, `BALL_MIN`=0.34, `MELT.HOLD`=2)
  3. **the ball becomes the comparator** - `then: morph` (the default): the ball's one ring is carried into the comparator's glyph rings by the pairing rule - by rank on centroid x, surplus rings born from the ball's own centre (trigger: MELT.BALL_END of `dur`, over the rest; dials: `DEGEN_R`=0.6, `MORPH_A.N`=96, `SIMPLIFY`=1.2)
  4. **the ball bursts or is thrown** - `then: splash`: melt.mjs's seeded splatter at the figure's scale, drying as the hand writes. `then: throw`: the ball sits in its weight and is thrown off, the hand writing after melt's release (trigger: MELT.BALL_END of `dur`; dials: `MELT.BURST_END`=0.40, `MELT.ANTIC`=0.22, `MELT.TO`=[1.02, 1.32])
  5. **the quoted figure is taken away** - `form: streak` (P57 T12b): the glyphs melt where they stand, each a drip opening in the hand's reading order on melt's run. `form: collapse`: the hand takes the ink back, last glyph first (trigger: the chart_to's `at`, over TAKE_SHARE of `dur`; dials: `TAKE_SHARE`=0.55, `MELT_BOX`=3.2)
  6. **the hand re-writes the comparator** - on every form but the morph, the comparator's `text` is written at the SAME DATUM by the figure's own hand (species/figure.mjs's write clock), glyph after glyph (trigger: TAKE_SHARE of `dur` (streak, collapse), the ending's own release (splash, throw); dials: `FIGURE.WRITE`=0.6)
  7. **the numeral counts** - `form: count` (E60's counter, the form P57 T12 shipped): the quoted numeral runs to the comparator's on min-jerk, in the metric's own clothes (trigger: the chart_to's `at`; dials: `COUNT`=0.72)
  8. **the words cross** - `form: count`: the text either side of the numeral crosses THROUGH ZERO - the metric's clothes become the comparator's where there is nothing on screen to swap (trigger: SWAP of `dur`, over CROSS of it; dials: `SWAP`=0.5, `CROSS`=0.2)
  9. **the label is written** - the comparator's `label` - what the number MEANS - is written beneath by the hand (trigger: LABEL_AT of `dur`; dials: `LABEL_AT`=0.55)
  10. **the metric holds beside it** - `hold: "metric"` stands the quoted figure beside the comparator at GHOST_A of the ink and GHOST_F of the size; `hold: "gone"` gives the comparator the stage (trigger: once the comparator has landed; dials: `GHOST_A`=0.62, `GHOST_F`=0.74, `GAP`=14)
- **options** `melt` form: THE DEFAULT - the quoted figure's outlines sag on melt.mjs's law and BALL UP into one disc that holds the ink's own area; `then` says what becomes of the ball (COMPARE_FORMS); `streak` form: P57 T12b's text melt - the glyphs drip where they stand under melt's words streak, then the hand re-writes the comparator (COMPARE_FORMS); `collapse` form: the hand takes the quoted figure's ink back first (the undraw law), then re-writes the comparator (COMPARE_FORMS); `count` form: E60's counter - the numeral counts to the comparator's and the words cross through zero (P57 T12's form, kept as a setting) (COMPARE_FORMS); `morph` then: THE DEFAULT - the ball's one ring is carried by morph_a into the comparator's glyph rings (one ring into N), and the figure's <text> takes over at u = 1 (COMPARE_THENS); `splash` then: the ball bursts onto the page (melt.mjs's seeded splatter) and the hand writes the comparator through the drying ink (COMPARE_THENS); `throw` then: the ball sits in its own weight and is thrown off the page (melt.mjs's flight), and the hand writes the comparator after it (COMPARE_THENS); `metric` hold: the quoted metric stays legible beside the comparator (the default) (COMPARE_HOLDS); `gone` hold: the quoted metric leaves and the comparator has the stage (COMPARE_HOLDS)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/compare.mjs` - symbol `paintCompare` - also `compareFrame`, `compareMorphFrame`, `compareBallFrame`, `compareForm`, `compareThen`, `compareShape`, `comparePairGroups`, `compareBallR` - species/compare.mjs on PAGE_PAINTERS.compare, after the figures it morphs. P57 T12c (E76 s5): the default is composed from contour.mjs, morph_a.mjs, melt.mjs and figure.mjs
- **dials** `COMPARE` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/compare.mjs`: `TAKE_SHARE`=0.55, `MELT_BOX`=3.2, `COUNT`=0.72, `SWAP`=0.5, `CROSS`=0.2, `LABEL_AT`=0.55, `GHOST_A`=0.62, `GHOST_F`=0.74, `GAP`=14, `SUB_DY`=1.3, `WIDTH_EM`=0.56, `PAD`=4, `OVERLAP`=1.6, `RASTER_S`=4, `RASTER_A`=0.5, `RASTER_PAD`=4, `SIMPLIFY`=1.2, `DEGEN_R`=0.6, `BALL_SWELL`=1.0, `BALL_MIN`=0.34, `SAG_GAIN`=3.2, `SEED`=7
- **status** wired (backlog R26-70) - **callable** yes
- **proof** golden compare-morph - test content/video_engine/tests/kinetics/compare.test.mjs - first use none
- **doctrine** E76 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2442; E76 s5 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2442; E73 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2342; E88 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2661; E77 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2480; E60 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1959; E50 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1520

### The extend chart-to verb

- **id** `chart_to:extend` - **does** New points or a later series draw on at the pen after the axes retarget.
- **when** more of the same series ('and then May') or a later series of the same file ('then consumption') - drawn on at the pen (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py CHART_TO_WHEN)
- **example** `{"kind": "chart_to", "at": 1.0, "dur": 1.0, "to": "extend", "to_index": 5}` (content/video_engine/tests/test_chart_transitions.py:363; key: chart_to {"to": "extend"}; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **axes retarget** - the axes rescale to the grown window first (trigger: the chart_to's `at`; dials: `RESCALE`=0.45)
  2. **pen draws on** - the new points draw on at the pen (trigger: RESCALE share of `dur`)

### The morph-to chart-to verb (ARAP)

- **id** `chart_to:morph` - **does** The area under one line becomes another line's area mid-page by as-rigid-as-possible morphing.
- **when** a different LINE series in the same frame ('what the Fed charges against what America pays') - the area under the line becomes the target's by ARAP (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py CHART_TO_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'chart_to', 'at': 12.0, 'dur': 2.0, 'to': 'morph', 'state': 1}` (authored; key: chart_to {"to": "morph"}; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **line leaves** - the standing line leaves (trigger: the chart_to's `at`; dials: `LEAVE`=0.3)
  2. **area morphs** - the area under the line becomes the target's by ARAP (trigger: LEAVE share of `dur`)
  3. **target builds** - the target line builds over the morphed area (trigger: at + dur; dials: `BUILD`=3.0)
- **blend** 43 s43.5 ARAP -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/43-SCENE-GRAPH-AND-TRANSFORM.md:62)
- **blend** E64 morph, never cut -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2079)

### The park chart-to verb

- **id** `chart_to:park` - **does** The chart shrinks toward a corner of its own box to make room, and grows back on an un-park.
- **when** room for the next thing beside the chart (a card, a second diagram); scale 1.0 is the UN-PARK when the cards leave; it moves no data and restarts no clock (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py CHART_TO_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'chart_to', 'at': 36.18, 'dur': 0.9, 'to': 'park', 'scale': 0.55, 'anchor': 'top'}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:4; key: chart_to {"to": "park"}; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **park** - the active chart shrinks toward its anchor corner on min-jerk and holds (trigger: the chart_to's `at`)
  2. **un-park** - a later park to scale 1.0 grows it back (trigger: a later chart_to park with scale 1.0)

### The recast chart-to verb

- **id** `chart_to:recast` - **does** The same data changes form on the page - lines become bars by series, by tag, by data, or by a hand-over.
- **when** the same data in another form: keyed (n lines -> n bars by series, 'where the four stand today'; RECAST_PAIRS) or the hand-over (a line into the monthly bars, into a pie of the holders) (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py CHART_TO_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'chart_to', 'at': 50.3, 'dur': 1.4, 'to': 'recast', 'state': 1}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:7; key: chart_to {"to": "recast"}; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **words erase** - the standing sub and source erase (trigger: the chart_to's `at`; dials: `ERASE_S`=0.4)
  2. **standing chart leaves** - plain: the chart un-draws on segEase while its axes hand over; keyed: marks tween (tags appear over KEYED.TAG first); data-keyed: the data travel to their bars on a clock of at least MIN_S (trigger: `at`, over `dur`; dials: `TAG`=0.3, `MIN_S`=1.6)
  3. **new words write** - the arriving state's sub and source write (trigger: ERASE_S after `at`)
  4. **target builds** - plain recast: the named state draws on by its own build envelope (trigger: at + dur; dials: `BUILD`=3.0)
- **blend** E64 re-write, never cut -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2079)
- **blend** Bravos 99-105 keyed bars -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py:273)

### The whole-chart remake

- **id** `chart_to:remake` - **does** A full chart becomes a full chart on one clock - every series, datum, axis, label, title. line -> bars: each datum's column becomes its bar. bars -> line: the ink collapses to ONE point at the apex, then the line draws back to the root.
- **when** the sentence turns the SAME data into the other whole chart ('month by month, this is what it did') - every series, datum, axis, label and the title transform on one clock, line <-> bars; when only the scale, the window or the form changes, the verb is rescale, extend or recast (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py CHART_TO_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'chart_to', 'at': 12.0, 'dur': 2.4, 'to': 'remake', 'state': 1}` (authored; key: chart_to {"to": "remake"}; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **line -> bars: the history leaves** - the dash window slides from the line's start to the first keyed datum; the axes begin handing over (trigger: the chart_to's `at`; dials: `LEAVE`=0.28)
  2. **line -> bars: the ink drops in** - each keyed datum's share of the source's ink fills its column as the stroke over it leaves (trigger: LEAVE share of `dur`; dials: `INK`=0.35 of the travel)
  3. **line -> bars: the shapes travel** - every ring morphs into its bar by morph_a, the datum marks travel to their tops, the labels re-write (trigger: MOVE share of the travel; dials: `MOVE`=0.2 of the travel)
  4. **line -> bars: the bars take over** - the target's own rectangles take the landed rings' place and their numbers write at the landed data (trigger: TRAVEL share of `dur`; dials: `TRAVEL`=0.9)
  5. **bars -> line: the gather** - every bar's ring collapses into ONE point at the apex (the highest bar's top), the farthest leaving first and all landing together; the bars' own numbers un-write (trigger: the chart_to's `at`; dials: `GATHER`=0.24, `SPREAD`=0.25)
  6. **bars -> line: the point settles** - the gathered point carries itself to where the arriving line's own apex datum stands (trigger: GATHER share of `dur`; dials: `SETTLE`=0.32)
  7. **bars -> line: the line draws back** - the page's own stroke draws OUT of the apex to the root, nib live, while the axes finish handing over - never a whole line arriving (E99 s39) (trigger: SETTLE share of `dur`; dials: `HOLD`=0.86)
- **blend** E99 s34 a morph reads as a transformation, not a cut -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:3143)
- **blend** E64 the data-keyed recast (the datum travels, the axes hand over) -> the correspondence and the hand-over (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:455)
- **blend** E99 s39 a morph is proof we are manipulating the world, not tricking them -> the bars -> line ending: the collapse to the apex and the draw back to the root (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:3196)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `lpPaintRemake` - also `lpPaintRemakeToLine`, `lpRemakeDrawBack`, `lpRemakeFor`, `lpAxisHandOver` - lpPaintRemake (line -> bars) / lpPaintRemakeToLine + lpRemakeDrawBack (bars -> line, the page's own dash pair) + kinetics/chartxf.mjs's rings, clocks and windows
- **dials** `REMAKE` in `content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/chartxf.mjs`: `COLS`=8, `LEAVE`=0.28, `TRAVEL`=0.9
- **status** wired - **callable** yes
- **proof** golden remake-line-to-bars - test content/video_engine/tests/test_whole_chart_morph.py::test_at_u_050_neither_chart_is_drawable_as_itself - first use none
- **doctrine** E99 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2901; E99 s39 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2901; E64 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2079; E50 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1520
- **aliases** "the whole-chart morph" (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:3143)

### The rescale chart-to verb

- **id** `chart_to:rescale` - **does** The chart's axes retarget on one clock to the window the story is about.
- **when** the same series at another scale - 'since February', 'at full width': the window the story is about; never a window that drops the sentence's point (content/video_engine/scripts/build_scene_timeline_f.py CHART_TO_WHEN)
- **example** `{'kind': 'chart_to', 'at': 21.09, 'dur': 1.4, 'to': 'rescale', 'window': [2026.043, 2026.457]}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:4; key: chart_to {"to": "rescale"}; check: species)
- **phases**
  1. **marks re-project** - every mark moves from its place under scale A to its place under B on one eased clock (trigger: the chart_to's `at`, over `dur` (segEase))
  2. **old ticks leave** - ticks outside the new domain fade out by half the clock (trigger: `at`; dials: `LEAVE`=0.5)
  3. **new ticks arrive** - ticks of the new domain fade in over the second half (trigger: half the clock; dials: `ARRIVE`=0.5)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `lpPaintStates` - lpPaintStates / "rescale" + kinetics/chartxf.mjs
- **dials** `XF` in `content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/chartxf.mjs`: `LEAVE`=0.5, `ARRIVE`=0.5
- **status** wired - **callable** yes
- **proof** golden ledger-extend - test none - first use systems-and-blowups SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py (no approval record found for this build) t=21.09
- **doctrine** E58 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1854
- **aliases** "`rescale`: the chart's axes retarget" (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:106)

## overflow

### The breakthrough burst overflow

- **id** `overflow:burst` - **does** The bar that cannot fit builds to the comparator's level, then shoots to its number while the scale rewrites under it.
- **when** none
- **example** `{"overflow": "burst"}` (authored; key: series object (evidence/objects/*.series.json) "domain" + "overflow": "burst"; check: overflow)
- **phases**
  1. **build to the comparator** - the breaking bar builds to the tallest honest bar's level, a bar like the others (optional grey '?' track behind it) (trigger: the page build (build_s or LP.BUILD); dials: `BUILD`=3.0)
  2. **hold** - stands at the comparator's level; the optional placeholder track leaves (trigger: the build's end; dials: `BT_HOLD`=0.5, `PH_OUT_S`=0.18)
  3. **shoot + rescale** - the bar shoots to its true value while the scale rewrites, old ticks slide and fade, new ticks fade in, the pill counts; the glow ramps (trigger: BT_HOLD after the build (optionally stepped: break_cadence 'stop'); dials: `BT_RUN`=0.6, `BT_GLOW`=18)
  4. **overshoot settles** - the bar overshoots and settles back; the glow holds at half (trigger: BT_RUN elapsed; dials: `BT_OVER`=0.05, `BT_SETTLE`=0.3)
- **blend** Bravos 8:02.0-8:02.6 the SPR bar -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs:4817)
- **blend** E60 the breakthrough is a rescale -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1959)
- **blend** stop-action cadence blend (break_cadence) -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/breakthrough.mjs:1)
- **options** `break` Break (burst's alias): Identical to burst: `break` resolves to the burst mechanic. (OVERFLOW_MODES)

### The stepped overflow

### The press card dock

- **id** `dock_kind:press` - **does** A headline cropped from its screenshot lands in the stage's centre box with its masthead source line and the quoted phrase, underlined as it is said; alone it lands, holds and retracts.
- **when** the sentence QUOTES a headline or a claim with a masthead (row 1) - THEIR claim never pages (B1); one card for one claim, the stack when three claims land in a row (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:88)

  3. **retract** - past its exit the card holds the pose it left in and retracts on the dock's spring - never a dissolve (trigger: the dock's exit; dials: `DOCK_RETRACT_S`=0.35)
- **blend** Bravos shots 5-10 (their claims as cards on the plate) -> the card and its pile (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/press.mjs:2)

### The verdict stack

- **id** `dock_payload:stack` - **does** Proofs fly in from depth one at a time on their phrases, each held large near centre, then receding in READING ORDER to a rail spot; the LAST lands centre and stays; the wall gathers in around it, then bursts radially.
- **when** the sentence lists N proofs of one claim in a row ('three things say so') - each flies in on its word; a list, not one chart (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:169)
- **example** `("ev-holds-stack-v1",0,701.73,727.63)` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/steel-and-paper/SHOT-TABLE-F.py:136; key: dock META entry `species: "stack"` with `stack: {items: [{id, at}], clear_at}` in <build>/evidence-dock.json, docked by a shot-table tuple whose window runs past clear_at; check: dock)
- **phases**
  1. **enter** - the card flies in from depth, swinging in from the side, rotating flat and fading up, to its FOCUS pose (trigger: items[i].at - the item's verbatim beat (one card at a time); dials: `DUR_S`=0.9, `ease`=cubic out 1 - (1 - e)^3, `translateZ_from_px`=-700, `rotateY_from_deg`=30 x dir, `side_swing_px`=460 x dir, `rise_px`=90, `dir`=+1 for odd i, -1 for even i)
  2. **focus** - the card holds LARGE near stage centre while its phrase is spoken, drifting (sine wander + scale breathing) (trigger: between its own at and the next item's at; dials: `active_cx_px`=930 + 70 (odd i) / 930 - 70 (even i), `active_cy_px`=400 + (i mod 3) x 26, `active_width_px`=840 (scale = 840 / the rail width), `drift_x_px`=sin(t x 0.55 + i x 1.7) x (8 + 26a), `drift_y_px`=cos(t x 0.7 + i x 2.1) x (5 + 14a), `scale_breath`=sin(...) x 0.008 x a, `tilt_deg`=tilt x (1 - a) + sin(...) x 0.6 x a, `z_index`=9 while a > 0.5, else 7)
  3. **recede** - the card eases back to its RAIL SPOT - nine asymmetric spots around the page, the centre kept open - so the page fills as a mosaic around the talking card (trigger: items[i + 1].at - and the LAST proof never recedes (E99 s59): the wall ends on it, at the focus spot; dials: `DUR_S`=1.0, `ease`=in-out cubic, `SPOTS_left_top_width_pct`=[[2, 5, 24], [27, 3, 22], [51, 4, 22], [74, 5, 24], [1, 40, 22], [77, 40, 22], [4, 62, 26], [37, 66, 24], [68, 62, 26]], `layout`=16:9 - four across the top, the mid-frame flanks, three along the bottom; each row left to right, `layout_9x16`=READING BANDS (E99 s43): proofs 1-2 across the TOP band left to right, 3-4 across the BOTTOM, 5-6 top, 7-8 bottom - four pairs, the ninth proof at the CENTRE (E99 s59), the focus card large between the bands)
  4. **idle** - railed cards float almost still at their tilt - until the gather, where that float fades out (trigger: a = 0 (railed); dials: `drift_x_px`=sin(t x 0.55 + i x 1.7) x 8, `drift_y_px`=cos(t x 0.7 + i x 2.1) x 5, `tilt_deg`=[-3, 2, -2, 3, -2.5, 2.5])
  5. **gather** - the wall CLOSES: every railed card draws toward the centre card (sliding under it, z 7 beneath z 9) while its idle fades out, so the burst leaves a gathered wall and not the rails (trigger: clear_at - 0.9 (GATHER_LEAD) - the same lead Steel and Paper spends on its last station change; dials: `DUR_S`=0.9, `ease`=cubic out 1 - (1 - g)^3 - the reference's own ARRIVAL law (an in-out open is slower than the idle drift, and a gather slower than the drift IS drift), `pull`=0.22 of the card's own distance to the centre card (65-173 px measured on the two golden walls), `guard`=never past the centre card's CORE (0.5 of its rect) + 16 px, so no rail vanishes beneath it, `idle`=the railed drift/breath is multiplied by (1 - ease): the wall holds its breath as it closes, `reference`=Steel and Paper HAS no gather - its settled rails move a net -7.7 to +11.2 px in the 3.29 s before the burst (measured 2026-09-16), `goldens`=verdict-stack@proof-gather (19.29) and verdict-stack-9x16@proof-gather (15.79) - the wall mid-draw on both forms)
  6. **burst** - every card is thrown RADIALLY along its own bearing FROM ITS GATHERED POSE, spinning, scaling up and fading, staggered - the centre card has no outward bearing, so it goes straight down and at the viewer, last of the wall (trigger: clear_at - the pivot line; dials: `stagger_s`=0.035 (E99 s59 tighter: the Steel and Paper reference measures 0.06, nine cards over 0.48 s; 0.035 fires them over 0.28 s), `DUR_S`=0.42 (the reference's own is 0.50: the wall is gone 0.70 s after the clear instead of its measured 0.99 s), `ease`=ease-in quad (cb^2), `throw_px`=560 x bx, 420 x by (bx, by = the card centre's offset from stage centre over 700/1920 and 460/1080 of the stage), `translateZ_px`=340, `spin_deg`=tilt + 24 x dir, `scale`=1 -> 1.22, `opacity`=1 -> 0, `teardown`=the .stackbox is removed at t > clear_at + 1.4 s or t < dock enter - 0.5 s, `origin`=the card's OWN pose at clear_at - the gathered wall, so nothing snaps back to its rail on the first burst frame, `centre_card`=bx 0, by 1: straight down, plus the same translateZ - the verdict is the last thing to leave)
- **blend** remotion-bits transform3d-showcase (https://remotion-bits.dev/docs/bits/transform3d-showcase/) -> enter (the fly-in from depth) (recorded; docs/operator-ledger/LEDGER.jsonl:575; docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1375; docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs:3227)

### The test card

- **id** `chart_dock:checklist` - **does** A procedure table lands row by row on the narration: each question types on letter by letter, the other cells fade in, and the answer cells take a chisel-edged marker sweep; re-docked for under 12 s it replays as a recap.
- **when** the sentence walks a PROCEDURE (a question, its answer) - rows land keyed to the narration (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:189)
- **example** `("ev-test-scorecard-v1",0,492.3,543.0)` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/steel-and-paper/SHOT-TABLE-F.py:114; key: `checklist: {head: [...], rows: [{cells: [...], colors?}]}` in the dock asset's .series.json; check: chart)
- **phases**
  1. **fit** - the columns auto-fit once: natural widths measured, slack spread, over-long cells squeezed by textLength (trigger: the first drawChart tick; dials: `PAD_px`=26)
  2. **row lands** - each row starts on its narration-keyed delay (trigger: rows[i].delay from narration_key_delays; RECAP (dock hold < 12 s): i x 0.8 s; dials: `recap_hold_s`=12, `recap_row_step_s`=0.8)
  3. **question types** - cell 0 types on character by character (trigger: row start + 0; dials: `char_s`=0.045, `fade_s`=0.35)
  4. **cells fade** - cells 1-3 fade in on their offsets (trigger: row start + [0.6, 1.0, 1.6] s (recap [0.25, 0.45, 0.7]); dials: `fade_s`=0.35)
  5. **highlight sweep** - answer cells (column index >= 2) take a cap-height marker band swept left to right behind the text, its leading edge chisel-tilted (trigger: that cell's offset; dials: `sweep_s`=0.55, `ease`=cubic out, `skewX_deg`=-7, `band_h_px`=30, `band_opacity`=0.28 (a K-M ink layer when kinetics km_ink), `default_cell_colours`=["#f4f6f8", "#dce3ea", "#3bc9b0", "#ff8a8c"])
- **blend** remotion-ui marker highlight (cap-height band, chisel-tilt edge) -> highlight sweep (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs:3583)
- **blend** the typewriter (question cells TYPE on) -> question types (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs:3584)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/checklist.mjs` - symbol `paintChecklist` - also `CHECKLIST`, `buildChecklist` - P55 T7: out of fillDock's C.checklist build + drawChart's fit/row paint, goldens byte-identical; refused as a ledger PAGE (test_ledger_page.py)
- **dials** `CHECKLIST` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/checklist.mjs`: `COLORS`=Object.freeze(["#f4f6f8", "#dce3ea", "#3bc9b0", "#ff8a8c"]), `HEAD_DY`=18, `ROW0_DY`=64, `ROW_PITCH`=58, `ROW_DELAY_S`=3, `HL_FROM_COL`=2, `BAND_INSET`=8, `BAND_RISE`=21, `BAND_H`=30, `BAND_W0`=10, `BAND_RX`=4, `BAND_ALPHA`=0.28, `KM_GROUND`="#16181c", `RULE_DY`=18, `RULE_COLOR`="#24262b", `RULE_W`=1.5, `FIT_MARGIN`=28, `FIT_MIN_W`=40, `FIT_PAD`=26, `FIT_CHAR_W`=10, `FIT_X0`=64, `FIT_ROOM_SLACK`=8, `CHISEL_DEG`=-7, `RECAP_S`=12, `RECAP_ROW_S`=0.8, `OFFS`=Object.freeze([0, 0.6, 1.0, 1.6]), `RECAP_OFFS`=Object.freeze([0, 0.25, 0.45, 0.7]), `CELL_FADE_S`=0.35, `TYPE_S`=0.045, `SWEEP_S`=0.55, `SWEEP_W_FALLBACK`=300, `SWEEP_PAD`=18, `SWEEP_ROOM_PAD`=12

### The art-embed surface dock option

- **id** `dock_option:embed` - **does** The card lands ON a surface the plate declares, projected onto its four corners; a bare still or clip COVERS its surface by default, while a card carrying badges, a record, a chart or proofs stays a reflowed card (E95).
- **when** a press quote or a record lands in the ART world - on the poster, the TV, the laptop, the paper of an approved plate; never a chart. (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:31)
- **example** `{"embed": "tv", "press": {"source": "THE HERALD", "phrase": {"x0": 0.1, "y0": 0.2, "x1": 0.6, "y1": 0.4}}}` (content/video_engine/tests/test_art_embed_media.py:91; key: dock option {'embed': '<surface name in <plate>.layers.json embed>'}; check: dock_option)
- **phases**
  1. **enter** - a throw lands onto the plane with its own impact; any other arrival fades up in place (trigger: the dock's enter; dials: `FADE_S`=0.35)
  2. **darken** - the room outside the surface dims on one min-jerk move, the surface itself kept clear (trigger: the surface's declared `darken` word; dials: `DARK_S`=0.35, `DARK_ALPHA`=0.44, `DARK_HOLE`=0.58, `DARK_REACH`=1.25)
  3. **hold** - the card holds on the surface carrying its own idle about its centre (E49) (trigger: after enter)
  4. **screen** - on a SCREEN the plate's light returns over the card, the bezel falls off, the paper lifts; on a paper the card casts a small shadow (trigger: surface kind (declared, or the name matches EMBED_SCREEN); dials: `SHEEN`=0.4, `VIGNETTE`=0.045, `LIFT`=1.04, `SHADOW`=6)
- **blend** Bravos's TV world (their claims on a monitor in a lit studio) -> the surface (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs:2729)
- **options** `fit` Picture fit on a surface (cover | contain): A still or clip on a surface fills the surface's whole box (cover) or letterboxes on the screen's own dark (contain) - no paper, masthead or rail. (DOCK_OPTS); `fit:contain` Contain: The picture is letterboxed inside the surface on the screen's own dark ground (EMBED_FILL_GROUND #05131e).; `fit:cover` Cover: fills the whole surface box, cropped - the DEFAULT for a bare still or clip (E95); a card with badges, a record, a chart or a stack never covers, it reflows; `surface:paper` Paper surface (the default kind): The card LIES on the surface (a poster, a washi sheet, a paper on a desk) with its own small shadow.; `surface:screen` Screen surface: The surface DISPLAYS the card: the plate's own light comes back over it, the bezel's inner edge falls off, the card's paper lifts toward the light.; `target.kind:embed` A surface as a camera target: A camera move takes a declared surface as its target; the compiler writes the quad onto the target.
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `embedPaint` - also `paintEmbeddedPress`, `embedDark` - embedPaint + paintEmbeddedPress + embedDark; projection kinetics/homography.mjs; row: the plate's layers.json must declare the surface
- **status** wired - **callable** yes: probe OK; the plate must declare the surface
- **proof** golden art-embed - test content/video_engine/tests/test_art_embed_media.py - first use none
- **doctrine** CAPABILITIES ART-embed -> docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:32; E66 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2146; E86 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2621; E95 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2788; E59 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1903; BACKLOG R26-74 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:480

### The read-then-park dock option

- **id** `dock_option:read` - **does** A centred card first pops at a reading box, holds, then parks over a minimum-jerk layout move to its kept box beside the chart; its type scales with its width.
- **when** a card that must be READ, then KEPT beside the chart (docs/content-video-engine/SPECIES-BY-SENTENCE.md:88)
- **example** `{'centre': True, 'card_aspect': 1.0, 'centre_w': 0.4, 'centre_x': 0.77, 'centre_y': 0.335, 'read': {'centre_w': 0.58, 'centre_x': 0.5, 'centre_y': 0.535, 'card_aspect': 0.47}, 'read_s': 1.41, 'park_s': 0.7}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:6; key: dock option {'centre': True, 'read': {centre_w, centre_x, centre_y, card_aspect}}; check: dock_option)
- **phases**
  1. **pop** - the card pops at its reading box (trigger: the dock's enter)
  2. **hold** - holds at reading size (trigger: enter; dials: `read_s_default`=1.2)
  3. **park** - shrinks and slides to its kept box on a minimum-jerk layout move (trigger: enter + read_s; dials: `park_s_default`=0.7)
- **options** `park_s` park_s: The park's length, in seconds (default DOCK_PARK_S). (DOCK_OPTS); `read_s` read_s: The hold at the reading box, in seconds (default DOCK_READ_S). (DOCK_OPTS)

### The press pile

- **id** `dock_option:stack` - **does** Press cards flagged on one scene form a lit pile: the newest springs in while each older card is shoved one step back on a lean-and-squeeze pulse, fanning, shrinking and dimming; a card that leaves retracts and the pile closes.
- **when** the stack when three claims land in a row (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:88)
- **example** `{"press": {"kind": "press", "source": "The Herald, 4 Mar 2026", "phrase": {"x0": 0.12, "y0": 0.2, "x1": 0.66, "y1": 0.44}, "phrase_text": "the historic normal was never normal", "card": [1056, 313]}, "stack": True}` (content/video_engine/tests/test_press_dock.py:128; key: dock option {'press': <meta>, 'stack': True}; check: dock_option)
- **phases**
  1. **land** - the newest card springs down into the pile's box (trigger: the newest dock's enter; dials: `LAND_S`=0.5, `POP_FROM`=0.88, `DROP_PX`=22, `FADE_S`=0.12)
  2. **push** - every older card moves one step back, shoved sideways with a squeeze and a lean that return to zero (trigger: the same enter, on a shorter clock; dials: `PUSH_S`=0.38, `ease`=minimum jerk, `PUSH_DX_px`=26, `SQUEEZE`=0.035, `SKEW_DEG`=2.4, `pulse`=sin(pi p))
  3. **settle (the fan)** - each step behind sits higher, smaller and dimmer, floored so a deep card is still a card (trigger: push complete; dials: `STEP_PX`=30, `STEP_H`=0.22, `STEP_SCALE`=0.05, `BACK_SCALE`=0.78, `DIM`=0.22, `DIM_MIN`=0.34)
  4. **underline** - the newest card's phrase is underlined as it is said (trigger: a callout `underline` on the `phrase` target; dials: `UNDERLINE_EASE`=1 - (1 - u)^3)
  5. **retract** - a card past its exit retracts on the dock spring and the live count closes the gap (trigger: that dock's exit; dials: `DOCK_RETRACT_S`=0.35)
- **blend** doc 29 s9.27 Push hand-off (transitions-push) -> push (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:1809)
- **blend** doc 29 s9.27 Squiggle marks (hw-underline) -> underline (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/press.mjs:1)
- **blend** Bravos shots 5-10 -> the lit pile (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/press.mjs:2)
- **blend** Flash & Hogan minimum jerk -> push easing (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/press.mjs:19)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/species/press.mjs` - symbol `pressStack` - also `pressRest` - pressStack / pressRest (law) + engine pressPile / pressPose; compiler assign_press_stack; row: each pile member a press dock
- **dials** `PRESS` in `content/video_engine/scripts/species/press.mjs`: `LAND_S`=0.5, `POP_FROM`=0.88, `DROP_PX`=22, `FADE_S`=0.12, `PUSH_S`=0.38, `STEP_PX`=30, `STEP_H`=0.22, `STEP_SCALE`=0.05, `BACK_SCALE`=0.78, `DIM`=0.22, `DIM_MIN`=0.34, `PUSH_DX`=26, `SQUEEZE`=0.035, `SKEW_DEG`=2.4, `UNDERLINE_EASE`=3, `FACE`="house", `TYPE_MIN`=12, `TYPE_MAX`=220, `TYPE_Q`=0.25, `LINE_H`=1.08, `WORD_AIR`=0.012, `PROV_SHARE`=0.3, `PROV_GAP`=0.04, `PHONE_FLOOR`=17

### The field plate option (the page's ground)

- **id** `plate_option:field` - **does** Names the GROUND a page's charcoal arrives on: the soak (seeded stains through the paper), the two-plate cross-fade (the charcoal deckle faded over the cream page) or the scribble (the opt-in back-up); all leave by the soak's recede.
- **when** When we are trying to maintain continuity, connecting ideas, speaking across plates i think the cross-fade is the answer, when we are building an idea or introducing a new idea or looking to fill space to separate ideas, the soak is the transition. (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:3156)
- **example** `'ledger:ev-x:bars;field=soak'` (authored; key: `;field=soak|plates|scribble` on a ledger page (validator: page_field_spec); check: plate)
- **options** `soak` Seeded charcoal stains creep and then flood through the paper to the deckle (E22's signature). The engine's own default: a page that names no field takes it. (PAGE_FIELDS); `plates` The two generated plates: the charcoal filled to the deckle cross-fades over the cream page (doc 41's decided signature). Refused by name unless the page carries both plate and field_plate. (PAGE_FIELDS); `scribble` The BACK-UP: charcoal strokes written across the deckle one at a time with the nib at the front. Never a default, and nothing is built on it. (PAGE_FIELDS)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `buildLedger` - also `lpPlateRecede` - the field beat's build (mode = pg.field === scribble ? scribble : soak) and the two-plate path beside it; compiler page_field_spec / PAGE_FIELDS

### The narrative plate world

- **id** `plate_option:world` - **does** A generated still IS the scene: the plate fills the frame as the ground every dock lands on and every species fires over - `world.asset_id` with no `page` and no clip. The long form's default world.

### The throw arrival

- **id** `arrival:throw` - **does** The card is thrown in on a stepped arc and lands with its material's squash, dip and shadow.
- **when** none

  2. **impact** - the material's squash frames, the receiver dips, the contact shadow tightens (trigger: FLIGHT_S elapsed; dials: `IMPACT_SQUASH`=0.22, `DIP_PX`=4)
  3. **settle** - the material's spring (and restitution hop) brings it to rest (trigger: impact; dials: `SETTLE_S`=1.2)
  4. **eye goes to it (opt-in)** - camera attention scales toward the landed card and back (trigger: contact, when attention is on; dials: `SCALE`=1.06, `IN`=0.5, `OUT`=0.6)

- **options** `ink` Mass: ink: The house standard: one squash frame, a small rebound. (MASSES); `liquid` Mass: liquid: A spread with no rebound, overdamped. (MASSES); `metal` Mass: metal: A heavy rigid thing: no squash, a dead stop, a deep dip. (MASSES); `paper` Mass: paper: A card: one squash frame, a low hop, a flutter. (MASSES)

## camera

### The camera row (keys and attention)

- **id** `camera:keys` - **does** A scene's own camera: authored keys zoom and pan one persistent 2D similarity between key times on a named ease, or the attention law leans the frame toward each landed card; with neither the camera stays locked.
- **when** the stage is wider than the frame, or a move is tied to a landing (E51) - never a drift on a held chart; locked is the default (content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/camera.mjs)
- **example** `{'keys': [], 'attention': 'landings'}` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:4; key: shot row 8th element {'keys': [{t, s, look, ease}], 'attention': 'locked' | 'landings'} (validate_camera / validate_camera_row); check: camera)
- **phases**
  1. **lean in** - with attention=landings the camera leans toward the landed card (trigger: the dock's contact frame; dials: `SCALE`=1.06, `IN`=0.5)
  2. **hold** - held while the card is up (trigger: IN elapsed)
  3. **release** - eases back to identity before the card leaves (trigger: dock exit - OUT; dials: `OUT`=0.6)
- **options** `cubic` Camera ease: cubic: A camera key eases in by a cubic ease-out (the default). (CAMERA_EASES); `hold` Camera ease: hold: The previous key holds, then the camera steps to this key at its t. (CAMERA_EASES); `inout` Camera ease: inout: A camera key eases in and out symmetrically. (CAMERA_EASES); `landings` The attention pull: When a dock lands the camera leans 1.06 toward it from the contact frame, holds while the card is up, and releases before it leaves. (CAMERA_ATTENTION); `linear` Camera ease: linear: A camera key travels at constant speed. (CAMERA_EASES); `locked` The locked camera: The camera never moves on its own; with no keys it is the identity. (CAMERA_ATTENTION)
- **lives** module - `content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/camera.mjs` - symbol `camKeyState` - also `camAttentionState`, `camIdentity`, `camEase`, `ATTN` - no T1 row of its own: six rows (the four eases, the two attentions) named camera:keys as their parent
- **dials** `CAM` in `content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/camera.mjs`: `PUNCH_IN`=0.42, `PUNCH_OUT`=0.5, `PUNCH_SCALE`=1.14, `FOCUS_SCALE`=1.32, `PULL_FROM`=1.9
- **status** live - **callable** yes: the locked default runs on every build; keys and landings used only on the Tokyo test bed

### The blur-zoom scene exit

- **blend** the suck's shape, not inverted -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1429)

### The dip-through-black scene exit

- **id** `exit:dip` - **does** The outgoing scene ramps linearly to black over DIP_S/2 (0.47 s total), the boundary frame is black, and the incoming scene ramps back up; docks, captions and species ride the veil.
- **when** The dip is the natural default transition when the WORLD actually changes at the boundary - a different plate, clip or chart page arriving without a signature (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1450)
- **example** `(8.32, 17.42, 'plate-gates;idle=drift', (0, 0, 0), [], 'dip')` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/japan-tariff-trick/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:5; key: shot row 6th element 'dip' or 'dip:<s>' (validator: parse_exit (via scene_exit); melt boundaries: stamp_transition_pages/_melt_boundary); DEFAULT_EXIT_CHANGE; check: exit)
- **phases**
  1. **down** - the outgoing scene and everything above it ramps linearly to black (trigger: the next boundary minus DIP_S/2 (read from the incoming scene's exit); dials: `DIP_S`=0.47)
  2. **the cut in the black** - the boundary frame is fully black; the worlds switch inside it (trigger: the scene boundary)
  3. **up** - the incoming scene ramps linearly back from black (trigger: the scene boundary; dials: `DIP_S`=0.47)

- **blend** a plain ramp - the reference's dip is linear to the frame -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1431)
- **options** `dip` The dip's own length: The dip runs over the seconds its suffix declares instead of its default dial. (TIMED_EXITS)

### The dissolve scene exit

- **id** `exit:dissolve` - **does** The incoming scene cross-fades over the outgoing one over DISSOLVE_S 0.8 s (quartIO, or minJerk under the flag), the outgoing world keeps playing underneath.
- **when** none

### The evidence door scene exit

- **id** `exit:door` - **does** A card that landed flat swings open on one stage edge, away from the viewer, through the one projective path, onto the next world already mounted beneath it, until it is edge-on and gone.
- **when** A card's tilt is a MOTION, not a pose it jumps to: the card lands flat and fills the frame like a plate, then opens like a door onto the plate or chart behind it - on an evidence-free boundary. (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2889)
- **example** `'door:left'` (authored; key: shot row 6th element 'door', 'door:<left|right|top|bottom>' or 'door:<hinge>:<s>' (validator: parse_exit/_door_parts; out of a depth or plane page, or across a live dock: stamp_transition_pages/door_boundary_error); check: exit)
- **phases**
  1. **the swing** - the outgoing world rides above the incoming one and rotates about its hinge edge on min-jerk, projected at PAGE_DEPTH EYE 1.6 into a matrix3d prepended to its own transform, clipped to its stage rect (trigger: the scene boundary (a door arrives on the cut, like the slide); dials: `DOOR.S`=0.9, `DOOR.EYE`=1.6)
  2. **edge-on** - the swing ends at 180 - atan(d/h) degrees, where the eye lies in the card's plane: zero projected width, the back never shown, the incoming world alone (trigger: the boundary plus DOOR.S; dials: `DOOR.S`=0.9)
- **blend** doc 29 Part 6's 3D book-flip, a structural reframe on an evidence-free boundary (never built until E98 s7) -> the reframe itself and the evidence-free boundary refusal (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/29-EVIDENCE-MOTION-STANDARDS.md:243)
- **blend** the operator's own ask - "open it -- that effect essentially will look like opening an evidence door to the plate or chart behind" -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2889)
- **options** `door` The door's own length: the door runs over the seconds its last suffix declares (after the optional hinge), inside 0.45..1.8 s, instead of its default dial. (TIMED_EXITS); `left` The door: hinged on the stage's left edge - it opens toward the left and the next world shows from the right (the default). (DOOR_HINGES); `right` The door: hinged on the stage's right edge - the next world shows from the left. (DOOR_HINGES); `top` The door: hinged on the stage's top edge - it lifts away and the next world shows from below. (DOOR_HINGES); `bottom` The door: hinged on the stage's bottom edge - it falls away and the next world shows from above. (DOOR_HINGES)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `doorOn` - also `doorUNow`, `doorNamed`, `doorOpts`, `doorCss`, `DOOR` - the render loop's boundary block - THE DOOR (doorOn beside slideOn), painted after the slide; math in kinetics/transitions.mjs; compiler: _door_parts / door_boundary_error
- **status** draft (backlog R26-134) - **callable** yes: in SCENE_EXITS, TIMED_EXITS and WORLD_TAKING_EXITS; parse_exit accepts door[:<hinge>][:<s>] and the engine paints it (goldens door-open and its two proofs) - it awaits the operator's watch in motion
- **proof** golden door-open - test content/video_engine/tests/test_transitions_e47.py::test_the_door_swings_open_on_its_hinge_onto_the_incoming_world - first use none

### The melt scene exit

- **id** `exit:melt` - **does** Only the outgoing chart's ink sags, fuses and compiles on 2s into a dense heavy ball while the board stays; then one of four authored endings - thrown, splashed into the next chart or a plate, or handed to it as a morph.
- **when** Only the chart's information melts (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2659)
- **example** `(25.02, 33.97, 'ledger:ev-federal-load-v1:line:240:right:spiral:cut', (0, 0, 0), [], 'melt')` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/normal-for-which-bridge/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:6; key: shot row 6th element 'melt[:throw|:splash:chart|:splash:plate|:morph][:gather][:weight[:<material>]][:depth=<k>][:<s>][:<x>,<y>]' (validator: parse_exit (via scene_exit); melt boundaries: stamp_transition_pages/_melt_boundary); check: exit)
- **phases**
  1. **gather** - on `melt:gather` the sag is replaced: every mark and word on the page travels the page vortex's own map to the ball's centre and amasses there, turning to one ink; the point opens under them and vibrates (trigger: the scene boundary (u = 0), on `melt:gather` only - a melt without it renders the sag byte for byte; dials: `G_S`=0.9, `G_TURNS`=3.0, `G_LAG`=0.25, `G_R_FALL`=1.7, `G_ALPHA`=0.85, `G_SHRINK`=0.7, `G_KEEP`=0.10, `G_DEEP`=0.85, `G_LINE_N`=160, `G_CORE_AT`=0.55, `G_CORE_SEED`=0.30, `G_VIB`=3.0, `G_VIB_FALL`=0.45)
  2. **sag** - the chart's ink (never the board) smears downward in offset copies under the gooey threshold and fuses; its box outline grows seeded drips and slumps (trigger: the scene boundary (u = 0 of the exit window); dials: `S`=1.6, `MELT_END`=0.30, `DRIPS`=7, `SAG`=0.40, `TOP_SAG`=0.42, `BLUR`=26, `EDGE_SLOPE`=24, `RUN`=0.22, `RUN_COPIES`=6, `INK_BLUR`=4)
  3. **ball** - one CLOSING on 2s: the melting body's ring morphs to the circle while the ink squeezes into it, and the K-M-concentrated body is that same ring further along - so the circle is the END of a visible closing, never a disc arriving whole (trigger: it OPENS at FUSE_FROM of the sag and LANDS at FUSE_END of this phase, after which the ball stands (E99 s53: a rushed compile is a snap and is re-timed); dials: `BALL_END`=0.55, `BALL_R`=0.085, `HOLD`=2, `SQUEEZE`=1.15, `BALL_FUSE`=40, `FUSE_FROM`=0.55, `FUSE_END`=0.80, `FUSE_EASE`=0.15, `FUSE_RATE`=0.20, `FUSE_NEAR`=1.60, `BODY_FROM`=0.55, `BODY_TO`=0.86, `BODY_LEAD`=0.55, `INK_OUT`=0.62, `INK_DEEP`=3, `CORE`=12)
  4. **ending** - one of four authored endings - throw | splash:chart | splash:plate | morph (see melt_ending rows) (trigger: u = BALL_END (the ball's end))
  5. **pitch** - the ball is PICKED UP - pressed into the board, its weight sold, then lifted off it - and THROWN forward on a ballistic arc to the board's centre, its shadow a frame behind; the splat opens where it lands (trigger: u = the ball's end (after the weight phase when one is authored), on a `splash` ending only - every other ending has no pitch at all and its window is unchanged; dials: `T_S`=0.77, `PK_S`=0.32, `FL_S`=0.45, `T_MAX`=0.35, `T_AT`=0.5, 0.5, `T_LIFT_R`=0.55, `T_PRESS_PX`=6, `T_ARC`=0.18)
  6. **hand** - on `melt:morph` the ending IS the hand-over: the ball's ring is the next page's prop, its body wears the morphing outline, and its paint gives way to the page's own ink on that one shape (trigger: u = the ball's end (meltHandAt), on `melt:morph` only - and it runs for the rest of the window; dials: `M_S`=1.3, `M_FADE`=0.85)
  7. **weight** - the ball lands, rolls without slipping (the turn IS the distance over the radius), takes one nudge sold before it moves, and settles - its surface Rayleigh's modes, its ink mark turning with it, its highlight on the light (trigger: u = BALL_END of what is left (the ball's end), on `melt:weight` only; dials: `W_S`=1.15, `W_MAX`=0.55, `W_MASS`=metal, `W_DROP_PX`=34, `W_LAND`=0.20, `W_ROLL`=0.50, `W_SETTLE`=0.84, `W_ROLL_PX`=150, `W_ROLL_FRICTION`=2800, `W_NUDGE_PX`=46, `W_ANTIC_PX`=5, `W_ANTIC_S`=0.12, `W_MARK_AT`=0.62, `W_MARK_R`=0.30, `HL_SHEEN`=0.82)
  8. **gone** - the outgoing world, its ink and the overlay are hidden (trigger: u >= 1)
- **blend** HyperFrames' morph-text gooey threshold -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/melt.mjs:23)
- **blend** Kubelka-Munk ink concentration (kinetics/ink.mjs) -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/melt.mjs:28)
- **blend** stop-action stepped clock, impactSquash and throwXf (kinetics/stopaction.mjs) -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/melt.mjs:54)
- **blend** morph_a vertex outline morph -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/melt.mjs:56)
- **blend** the ink-bloom's ragged front -> the whole arc (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/species/melt.mjs:133)
- **blend** four laws we already owned, chained (R26-15's melt lesson) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:35)
- **options** `body` P61 T5c / E99 s49: the ball's BODY colour. Default (weight, no word) `reference` - the blueprint's near-black metal; `body=chart` is the ball that shipped, `body=slate` the board's ink #25313C. (MELT_BODIES); `depth` The melt at a depth: melt:...:depth=<k> puts the ink clone and the ball overlay on the camera read at k (camLayerCss), so the ball melts, lands and is thrown at that plane.; `gather` P61 T6 / E99 s2: the sag becomes the page vortex gathered to ONE point - every mark and word travels there and amasses on it, no blur, no wipe. Opt-in, composes with weight and any ending.; `melt` The melt's own length: The melt runs over the seconds its suffix declares instead of its default dial. (TIMED_EXITS); `morph` P61 T3 / R26-117: the melt ENDS at the ball and hands its one ring to the next page's page_enter:morph as that page's prop outline, on one clock - no cut between. (MELT_ENDINGS); `splash:chart` The melt: splash into the next chart: the ball is picked up, thrown forward and splats at the board's centre; the splatter forms the next chart, which arrives built. (MELT_ENDINGS); `splash:plate` The melt: splash that paints a plate: the ball is picked up and thrown at the board's centre, and a narrative plate springs up out of the splat, as if painted by that ink. (MELT_ENDINGS); `throw` The melt: thrown off: The ink ball is thrown off the stage and the next chart draws on the same board. (MELT_ENDINGS); `weight` The melt: the ball has MASS - it lands, rolls without slipping (its ink mark turning), is nudged and settles, its surface the living drop. Opt-in `melt:weight[:<material>]`, metal by default.
