### The slide scene exit

- **id** `exit:slide` - **does** The incoming frame pushes the outgoing one off the stage along one axis, both moving together by the same distance over SLIDE_S, each clipped to its own stage rect, so the two frames abut at every instant.
- **when** A HAND-OFF, not a world-taking transition: when the next world should read as the same picture carried on - chart to chart on the same board - so the two worlds stay spatially continuous. (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2641)
- **example** `'slide:left'` (authored; key: shot row 6th element 'slide:<left|right|up|down>' or 'slide:<dir>:<s>', optionally ending ':depth=<k_out>,<k_in>' (validator: parse_exit (via scene_exit); the direction is never defaulted and `push` is refused by name; a depth beside a depth page or a layered plate: stamp_transition_pages/slide_depth_world_error); check: exit)
- **phases**
  1. **the push** - the outgoing world travels sign * u * the stage's span and the incoming world (u - 1) * the same span, on min-jerk, each clipped to its own stage rect - one distance, opposite ends, so the two frames abut at every u (trigger: the scene boundary (a slide arrives on the cut, like the suck and the melt); dials: `SLIDE_S`=0.6)
  2. **the landing** - the outgoing frame is exactly off the stage and the incoming one stands at its own place; the page it hands to has been building on its axes throughout (trigger: the boundary plus SLIDE_S; dials: `SLIDE_S`=0.6)
- **blend** @remotion/transitions' `slide` presentation (the editor already imports the package - editor/src/TransitionEvidence60sProof.tsx) -> the geometry: the enter and the exit translate are one pair, one distance, opposite ends (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:481)
- **blend** the operator's own ask - "literally pushing out one frame with the next, so that you keep some of that congruency" -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2629)
- **options** `depth` The slide through the depth: slide:<dir>[:<s>]:depth=<k_out>,<k_in> - the outgoing world goes 1 -> k_out as it leaves, the incoming k_in -> 1 as it lands (camDepthSwap).; `slide` The slide's own length: the slide runs over the seconds its SECOND suffix declares (after the direction) instead of its default dial. (TIMED_EXITS); `left` The slide: pushed off to the left - the incoming frame arrives from the right. (SLIDE_DIRECTIONS); `right` The slide: pushed off to the right - the incoming frame arrives from the left. (SLIDE_DIRECTIONS); `up` The slide: pushed off upward - the incoming frame arrives from below. (SLIDE_DIRECTIONS); `down` The slide: pushed off downward - the incoming frame arrives from above. (SLIDE_DIRECTIONS)

- **status** draft (backlog R26-75) - **callable** yes: in SCENE_EXITS and TIMED_EXITS; parse_exit accepts slide:<left|right|up|down>[:<s>] and the engine paints it (goldens slide-mid / slide-landed) - it awaits the operator's watch in motion before a short ships it

### The suck scene exit

- **id** `exit:suck` - **does** The outgoing world collapses into a stage point, spinning SUCK_TURN 240 deg while it scales down over SUCK_S 0.3 s, riding above the incoming plate.

### The wipe scene exit

### The axes page enter

  2. **build** - the chart draws from the first frame (trigger: the scene boundary; dials: `BUILD`=3.0)

### The two-plate cross-fade field (a page keeping continuity)

- **id** `page_enter:field_plates` - **does** Beat 3 of the page's arrival, the decided signature: the generated charcoal-filled deckle fades in over the generated cream page on the field clock; the ground arrives smooth and whole and the chart draws inside the deckle's board.
- **when** When we are trying to maintain continuity, connecting ideas, speaking across plates i think the cross-fade is the answer, when we are building an idea or introducing a new idea or looking to fill space to separate ideas, the soak is the transition. (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:3156)

  1. **the inked plate arrives** - the charcoal filled to the deckle fades in over the cream page on expoOut of the field clock (trigger: the roll and the savor behind it; dials: `FIELD`=2.4)

### The soak field (a page introducing an idea)

- **id** `page_enter:field_soak` - **does** Beat 3 of the page's arrival: seeded charcoal stains creep and then flood through the cream to the deckle, deepening where they overlap, and end on the crisp rect. The eye has more to watch than a cross-fade gives it.
- **when** When we are trying to maintain continuity, connecting ideas, speaking across plates i think the cross-fade is the answer, when we are building an idea or introducing a new idea or looking to fill space to separate ideas, the soak is the transition. (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:3156)
- **example** `'ledger:ev-x:bars;field=soak'` (authored; key: `;field=soak` on a ledger page - or no field at all: the soak is the engine's own default; check: none)
- **phases**
  1. **the stains creep** - each seeded stain grows from its own lag on the field clock, deepening where two overlap (trigger: the roll and the savor behind it (the scene's first frame on a mount); dials: `FIELD`=2.4, `SEEPS`=9)
  2. **the ground closes** - the crisp charcoal rect takes over the last fifth, so the ground is flat before the title inks (trigger: b 0.78 of the field clock; dials: `rect`=0.78 -> 1.0)

### The morph page enter

- **id** `page_enter:morph` - **does** The page's prop outline - a named shape, a PLANTED element's traced silhouette, or the melt's own ball - deforms by ARAP into the area under its series while the page's GROUND arrives under it by its field entry; then the build lands.
- **when** The morph (P47 T3) is that device. (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1485)
- **example** `'ledger:ev-x:line:5:right:morph=2.5:cut'` (authored; key: ledger id 6th part ':morph' or ':morph=<s>' + ';morph=<shape>' (validator: parse_ledger_id (after split_plate_opts)); SIGNATURE_ENTERS; check: enter)
- **phases**
  1. **the source** - the prop outline is a named shape placed on the target's centroid and axis, or a traced poly / the melt's ball left exactly where it stood, re-expressed as a strip by arap.polyStrip (trigger: the first frame the morph paints; dials: `STRIP.EPS_X`=0.004, `STRIP.MIN_H`=0.02, `MORPH.COLS`=48)
  2. **the ground arrives** - the charcoal arrives by the field entry the row names (field=soak by default): the nearest stain seeded on the prop's centroid, the rest by distance, over MORPH.GROUND of the morph - never painted whole around the prop (E99 s52) (trigger: the page's first frame; dials: `MORPH.GROUND`=0.75, `MORPH.SEED_LAG`=0.6, `MORPH.SEED_R`=0.6, `MORPH.SEED_POW`=0.7)
  3. **the prop deforms** - the tab/plate/card outline deforms by ARAP into the chart's shape (trigger: the scene boundary; dials: `MORPH.S`=2.0)
  4. **build** - the page's build lands after the morph (its clock is offset by morph_s) (trigger: morph_s elapsed; dials: `BUILD`=3.0)
- **blend** ARAP / the object->chart morph (doc 43 s43.5) -> the whole arc (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/43-SCENE-GRAPH-AND-TRANSFORM.md:62)
- **options** `card` Morph prop: card: The page is born from a card outline with eased corners. (MORPH_SHAPES); `field` E99 s52: the GROUND a morph page arrives on - `;field=soak|plates|scribble`. Stamped `soak` when the row names none: the prop is already ink, so the ink spreads out of it. (PAGE_FIELDS); `hand` P61 T3 / R26-117: the poly the ENGINE hands in under `exit: melt:morph` - the ball's ring. The page's own ink rises under the ball over MELT.M_FADE instead of appearing over it.; `morph` The morph's prop outline: Names the prop outline (tab, plate, card) a morph-entering page starts from. (PLATE_OPTS); `plate` Morph prop: plate: The page is born from a plate outline with eased corners. (MORPH_SHAPES); `poly` P48 T5b / R26-16: world.morph = {poly: [[x, y], ...]} in stage fractions - a PLANTED element's own silhouette, traced by kinetics/contour.mjs. Refused under 3 points, self-crossing, off stage.; `tab` Morph prop: tab: The page is born from a tab outline (the default prop). (MORPH_SHAPES)

- **status** wired - **callable** yes: compiles; paints only behind kinetics.arap_morph, else falls back to a mount

### The cream mount page enter

- **id** `page_enter:mount` - **does** On the cut the page's cream is the ground at once (the outgoing world is gone, R26-50), the field soaks in over mount_s while the page rises on a 5-step staircase, then the title inks, the board punches in and the chart builds.
- **when** The mount (a page arriving for the first time) (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1433)
- **example** `'ledger:ev-parts-cascade-v1:bars:0:right:mount=0.8:cut;idle=live'` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/japan-tariff-trick/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:6; key: ledger id 6th part ':mount' or ':mount=<s>' (validator: parse_ledger_id (after split_plate_opts)); SIGNATURE_ENTERS; check: enter)
- **phases**
  1. **the cream lands** - the page's cream is the ground from the scene's first frame; the outgoing world is cut, never cross-faded (R26-50) (trigger: the scene boundary)
  2. **the soak rises** - the field soaks in while the page rises on a stepped staircase (trigger: the scene boundary (mount window = the soak); dials: `MOUNT_STEPS`=5, `LP_MOUNT_RISE`=28, `mount_s default (LP.FIELD)`=2.4)
  3. **ink** - title and source write per glyph once the field has soaked (trigger: the soak's end (mount_s); dials: `INK`=2.0)
  4. **punch-in** - the board punches in (trigger: the ledger clock (beat 5); dials: `PUNCH`=0.5)
  5. **build** - the chart builds onto its exact value strings (trigger: the ledger clock (beat 6); dials: `BUILD`=3.0)
- **blend** the soak (paper taking ink) -> ink (recorded; docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs:6698)
- **blend** the mount is a build, not a transition -> build (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1477)

### The spiral page enter

- **id** `page_enter:spiral` - **does** A page that was already drawn returns by running the vortex backwards over 1.6 s from its point - no roll, soak, ink or build.
- **when** the spiral (a page returning) (docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1433)
- **example** `'ledger:ev-federal-load-v1:line:240:right:spiral:cut'` (content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/normal-for-which-bridge/SHOT-TABLE-SHORT.py:6; key: ledger id 6th part ':spiral' (validator: parse_ledger_id (after split_plate_opts)); SIGNATURE_ENTERS; check: enter)

  2. **colours unwind** - every glyph, bar, pill and series point unwinds from the vortex point along the closed-form map (trigger: ui 0.4 - 1.0 of LP_RETRACT.IN (overlapping the stains); dials: `IN`=1.6, `TURNS`=3.0, `LAG`=0.25)
  3. **holds built** - the page stands drawn - its roll, soak, ink and build clocks are all behind it (trigger: the unwind's end)

### The vortex retract page exit

## caption

### The anchor caption mode

- **id** `caption:anchor` - **does** A caption page whose first word falls while a dock is live is stamped anchor: under a card it keeps its stage size in the band the page leaves free (E62) and drops to the quiet lower third only when no band fits.
- **when** the stage size in the free band under a card, quiet only when no band fits (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:22)
- **example** `cap_mode: 'anchor'` (authored; key: compiler-stamped `cap_mode: "anchor"` (no row key); check: none)
- **options** `caption:anchor` Page-pinned anchor caption: A ledger page pins its captions to the anchor strip for its whole span (a host plate's quiet zone is the host's).; `caption_band` The caption's band under a card: The compiler writes, on each dock entry, the band the page leaves free of the card; the player moves the stage-size strip there.; `quiet` Quiet caption: The static lower-third strip: smaller, a single fade, keywords still coloured, no punch - now only when no band holds the stage-size caption clear of the card (48 px / 800 floor on a… (CAPTION_BAND_ORDER)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `capPinned` - also `carded` - render caption block

### The phrase caption style

- **id** `caption:phrase` - **does** The whole caption page lands as one readable phrase (a 30 ms stagger), every word legible at once; only keywords re-pop and take the red box at their spoken time; landed words boil.
- **when** a short - the strip is read as one phrase, never chased word by word (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:59)

### The STAGE caption mode

- **id** `caption:stage` - **does** With no card up the caption IS the motion: centred in the page's quiet zone at 64 px / 800, each word popping at its own spoken time with a settling tilt, keywords in the accent.
- **when** no dock is up and the plate or page holds - the words carry the motion (docs/content-video-engine/CAPABILITIES.md:23)
- **example** `caption_modes: ['stage', 'anchor']` (authored; key: compiler-stamped `cap_mode: "stage"` per caption page + timeline `caption_modes: ["stage", "anchor"]` (no row key); check: none)
- **phases**
  1. **pop** - each word enters at its own spoken time, scale 1.16 -> 1.0, its alternating tilt settling to 0 (trigger: word.s - 0.05; dials: `STAGE_POP_S`=0.2, `from_scale`=1.16, `tilt_deg`=2.5)
  2. **lift** - the spoken word lifts while the voice is on it (trigger: word.s <= t < word.e; dials: `lift`=1.06)
  3. **band move** - under a card the strip keeps its size and moves to the band the compiler wrote (E62); quiet only when no band fits (trigger: a live dock)
- **blend** The caption Steel and Paper shipped (STAGE mode, doc 29 s9.25 #2, P34 T5) -> the blend's scale, tilt and opacity - the pop, one notch stronger, leading (recorded; docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md E90)
- **blend** The staggered fade-up envelope (P52 T10) [DERIVED: HyperFrames staggered-fade-up] -> the blend's rise - the minimum-jerk y the word is carried up on, under the pop (recorded; content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/stagger.mjs)
- **options** `fade_up` The staggered fade-up: The page's words rise 22 px through a 5 px blur from scale 0.92 on one minimum-jerk envelope over 0.34 s, 0.055 s apart - a wave, not a queue of pops. (CAPTION_ARRIVALS); `life:blend` The life setting `blend` (E90 s2, a blend not a switch): the pop's scale and tilt ride ON TOP of the stagger's rise, the pop leading by its 0.05 s anticipation, the held page breathing under it (E49). (CAPTION_LIVES); `life:pop` The life setting `pop`: the shipped stage pop one notch stronger - scale 1.22 -> 1.0 on the spring (LIFE.POP_LEAD against the base's 1.16), the alternating tilt settling as before. (CAPTION_LIVES); `life:stagger` The life setting `stagger`: P52 T10's envelope alone under each word - rise 22 px, scale 0.92 -> 1, blur 5 px -> 0 over 0.34 s, no tilt. The quiet register, on the word's own spoken time. (CAPTION_LIVES); `pop` The pop: Each stage word pops in at its own spoken time - the arrival every shipped build uses (no field is written). (CAPTION_ARRIVALS)
- **lives** inline - `docs/content-video-engine/samples/scene-evidence-engine.mjs` - symbol `stagePop` - also `carded`, `lifeAt` - render caption block + the template's `#caption.stage` CSS
- **dials** `LIFE` in `content/video_engine/scripts/kinetics/stagger.mjs`: `POP_LEAD`=1.22, `POP_S`=0.20, `ANTICIP_S`=0.05, `OPACITY_K`=2, `MP`=0.04
- **status** live - **callable** yes: every compiled build

## Recipes

A recipe is the unit of AUTHORING (E96): ordered card ids with offsets, the act it serves, the dials as
measured and the played instant that proves it. `proven` means the members fire inside `window_s` at that
instant in the timeline named below - `effects_catalog_check.py` re-walks it. A recipe that fires once is
a decoration; one that fires four times is a grammar.

| status | recipes | members | fires (sum of count) |
|---|---|---|---|
| proven | 15 | 59 | 94 |
| candidate | 30 | 115 | 0 |

### The badge ladder

- **id** `recipe:badge-ladder` - **does** A proof card lands, then its qualifications stamp onto its rail one per clause: the first 2.05 s after the card arrives, every later one 1.30 s behind the last.
- **acts** QUOTES, EXPLAINS - **window** 6s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `dock_kind:image` (The image dock) - the proof card lands over the world and holds - the claim's evidence arrives before any qualification
  - +2.05s -> `dock_option:badge` (The badge rail) - the first badge stamps onto the card's rail, 2.05 s after it lands
  - +3.35s -> `dock_option:badge` (The badge rail) - the second badge stamps one clause later, 1.30 s behind the first - optional
  - +4.65s -> `dock_option:badge` (The badge rail) - the third badge stamps on the next clause, the same 1.30 s beat - optional
  - +5.95s -> `dock_option:badge` (The badge rail) - the fourth badge stamps the last qualification, still on the 1.30 s beat - optional
- **dials** `FIRST_BADGE_S`=2.05, `BADGE_GAP_S`=1.3 (41 of 43 Steel docks, every first badge at dock_enter + 2.05 exactly; 40 gaps all exactly 1.30)
- **proof** steel-and-paper / build-f @ 50.4s - members at 50.4, 52.45, 53.75, 55.05, 56.35 - `content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/steel-and-paper/build-f/steel-and-paper.timeline.json`
- **status** proven - **count** 41 (a grammar) - **source** recipes_r1 R1; docs/research/runs/p56-recipe-seeds/seeds.jsonl seed R1
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; BACKLOG R26-103 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:535
- **aliases** "The badge ladder (2.05 / 1.30)" (docs/research/runs/grill_pipeline-value/recipes_r1.md:74)

- **acts** QUOTES, EXPLAINS - **window** 6s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `dock_kind:image` (The image dock) - the proof card lands over the world and holds - the evidence arrives before any qualification
  - +0s -> `dock_option:embed` (The art-embed surface dock option) - the card on a surface the plate declares, projected by kinetics:homography's four corners
  - +2.05s -> `dock_option:badge` (The badge rail) - the first badge stamps onto the card's rail, 2.05 s after it lands
  - +3.35s -> `dock_option:badge` (The badge rail) - the second badge stamps one clause later, 1.30 s behind the first
- **status** candidate - **count** 0 (unfired) - **source** recipes_r1 s4 (R1 slot)
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; BACKLOG R26-103 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:535

### The bare plate that lives

- **id** `recipe:bare-plate-that-lives` - **does** A plate with no evidence still moves: its cutouts step at 10 fps, the figure breathes asymmetrically, a card sits behind the plate's own foreground, and the outgoing world plays on under the next one.
- **acts** EXPLAINS, SETS - **window** 8s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `plate_option:world` (The narrative plate world) - the bare plate is the world the sentence is made on - no page, no chart
  - +0s -> `plate_option:use` (The use plate option) - names each Steel plate landing / bridge / reset (lint only)
  - +0s -> `species:plate_life` (The plate life species) - the 10 fps cutout boil for the 32 Steel plates that carry no dock
  - +0.2s -> `idle:figure` (The figure idle) - the asymmetric breath for a human plate (doc 48 s48.4)
  - +2.6s -> `dock_option:behind` (The behind-the-plate dock option) - depth by occlusion - the plate's foreground over the card
  - +6.2s -> `exit:dissolve` (The dissolve scene exit) - the outgoing world keeps playing under the breather
- **status** candidate - **count** 0 (unfired) - **source** recipes_r1 s4 (R11 slot)
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; BACKLOG R26-103 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:535

- **acts** COMPARES, RANKS - **window** 6s

  - +0s -> `page_builder:bars` (The bars ledger page) - the story bars page the form is applied to: its bars grow in stagger with their values landing
  - +0s -> `plate_option:form` (The form plate option (2.5D chart forms)) - each bar a prism, one light, a hard-edge shadow, every label where the flat bar puts it

### The card becomes the chart

- **id** `recipe:card-becomes-the-chart` - **does** The prop you were just handed IS the evidence page: a thrown card lands on the plate, the world dips, and the chart grows out of that card's own rectangle to fill the stage.
- **acts** COMPARES, QUOTES - **window** 6s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `idle:drift` (The drift idle) - the world plate holds and drifts its few pixels - the ground the prop is about to land on
  - +0.82..4.12s -> `arrival:throw + paper` (The throw arrival) - the prop card is thrown in with a paper mass and lands one second before the scene ends
  - +1.82..5.12s -> `exit:dip` (The dip-through-black scene exit) - the world dips through black on the boundary frame, the landed card riding the veil
  - +1.82..5.12s -> `page_enter:snap` (The card-then-snap page enter) - the ledger page grows out of that same card's rectangle - `snap_from` names the dock the viewer was just shown
- **proof** japan-tariff-trick / build-short @ 0s - members at 0, 0.82, 1.82, 1.82 - `content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/japan-tariff-trick/build-short/japan-short.timeline.json`
- **status** proven - **count** 2 (a grammar) - **source** recipes_r1 R6; docs/research/runs/p56-recipe-seeds/seeds.jsonl seed R6
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; BACKLOG R26-103 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:535; E61 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1991; E49 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:1494

### The pen carries on to the next month

- **id** `recipe:chart-extends-at-the-pen` - **does** The chart does not cut: the line reaches the number the sentence named, the axes retarget, and the next month draws on at the same pen - one page, two data states.
- **acts** SPANS, EXPLAINS - **window** 8s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `plate_option:then` (The then plate option (the next chart state)) - declares the second chart state a `chart_to` verb reaches
  - +0.4s -> `page_species:build_to` (The build-to page species) - the line builds along the page to the datum the sentence has just named
  - +3.4s -> `page_species:chart_to` (The chart-to page species) - the page-species carrier for all five verbs above
  - +3.4s -> `chart_to:extend` (The extend chart-to verb) - the pen carries on - "and then May" after `build_to` reaches its datum, on kinetics:chartxf's one clock
  - +6.2s -> `page_species:figure` (The written figure page species) - the hand writes the figure the extension arrived at
- **status** candidate - **count** 0 (unfired) - **source** recipes_r1 s4 (R10 slot)
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; BACKLOG R26-103 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:535

  - +0s -> `page_enter:morph` (The morph page enter) - the prop outline ARAPs into the chart shape on kinetics:morph_a

  - +2.6s -> `page_species:chart_to` (The chart-to page species) - the page-species carrier for all five verbs above
  - +2.6s -> `chart_to:morph` (The morph-to chart-to verb (ARAP)) - the ARAP hand-off between two line series in one frame (kinetics:arap under kinetics:chartxf's clock)
- **status** candidate - **count** 0 (unfired) - **source** recipes_r1 s4 (R6 slot)
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; BACKLOG R26-103 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:535

### The chart parks and their claim lands beside it

  - +4.4s -> `dock_option:stack` (The press pile) - the press pile - R4's pulse applied to external claims
  - +6s -> `species:newsreel` (The newsreel band species) - the sourced crawl under a surface that shows who said it

### The chart remade into the other chart

- **id** `recipe:chart-remade-into-the-other-form` - **does** The same data is re-drawn as the other whole chart with no cut: the standing chart is read, then every series, axis, label and the title travel on one clock into the other form, which lands and is held alive.

### The quoted figure becomes the number the viewer feels

- **acts** TURNS - **window** 12s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `page_enter:snap` (The card-then-snap page enter) - the page arrives - snapped from the landed card here, mounted on the cream on the other two

### The still-life breather

- **id** `recipe:still-life-breather` - **does** The beat between two arguments: no chart, one plate, and three still-life species alive in three corners of it for six seconds before the world is sucked away.
- **acts** SETS - **window** 6s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `plate_option:world` (The narrative plate world) - the held plate is the whole world of the beat - no page, no chart, one room the three species live in
  - +0s -> `species:steam` (The steam still-life species) - steam lifts off the cup on the held plate - the first of three things moving on a scene with no chart
  - +0.2s -> `species:ticker` (The ticker still-life species) - the ticker's rows flicker on the paper beside it, 0.2 s later
  - +0.3s -> `species:trace` (The trace hops species) - a trace draws across the third corner, so no region of the plate is still
  - +5.92s -> `exit:suck` (The suck scene exit) - the breather ends by collapsing into a point, spinning, and the argument resumes
- **proof** tokyo-tea-break / build-short.v2 @ 38.96s - members at 38.96, 38.96, 39.16, 39.26, 44.88 - `content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/tokyo-tea-break/build-short.v2/tokyo-short.timeline.json`

### The ground the page arrives on, chosen by its job

- **id** `recipe:the-page-ground-chosen-by-its-job` - **does** A page's ground is chosen by the beat's job: the cross-fade when the idea carries on from the plate before, the soak when the idea is new or two ideas are being separated - then the title inks and the chart builds.
- **acts** SETS, EXPLAINS - **window** 6s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `plate_option:field` (The field plate option (the page's ground)) - names the GROUND the page's charcoal arrives on; a page that names none takes the soak, the engine's own default
  - +0s -> `page_enter:field_plates` (The two-plate cross-fade field (a page keeping continuity)) - the cross-fade ground: the charcoal deckle fades over the cream page - the choice when the beat carries the idea on from the plate before - optional
  - +0s -> `page_enter:field_soak` (The soak field (a page introducing an idea)) - the soak ground: seeded stains creep and flood the cream to the deckle - the choice when a new idea starts or two ideas need separating - optional
  - +2.4s -> `page_enter:mount` (The cream mount page enter) - the arrival's ink beat - title and source write per glyph once the field has soaked (mount_s 2.4, INK 2.0); the card is the whole mount, the offset is its ink phase
  - +4.9s -> `page_builder:line` (The line ledger page) - the chart builds onto its exact value strings inside the deckle the field laid down (the line page here; any builder takes the same beat)
- **status** candidate - **count** 0 (unfired) - **source** P61 T4b goldens form-extruded-bar (the soak) and form-tilted-line (the cross-fade) - no approved cut has carried it
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; E99 s35 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2901; E22 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:614

  - +5.6s -> `exit:blurzoom` (The blur-zoom scene exit) - a world change between two of the same kind, on kinetics:ease's min-jerk

### The trace-callout ladder

- **id** `recipe:trace-callout-ladder` - **does** One number is struck at place after place on a bare picture: a hop draws, its label writes 0.55 s later, and the pair repeats every 1.26 s until the whole route is named.
- **acts** NAMES - **window** 6s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `species:trace` (The trace hops species) - the route hops from the first named place to the second, drawn in one stroke - defaults `bow`=0.16, `draw_s`=0.55, `width`=9
  - +0.55s -> `species:callout` (The scribble callout species) - the scribble names what was struck there, 0.55 s behind the hop - defaults `label_scale`=2.2
  - +1.26s -> `species:trace` (The trace hops species) - the next hop leaves 1.26 s after the last one, back across the picture - defaults `bow`=0.16, `draw_s`=0.55, `width`=9
  - +1.81s -> `species:callout` (The scribble callout species) - the same number is written again at the second place - defaults `label_scale`=2.2
  - +2.52s -> `species:trace` (The trace hops species) - the third hop keeps the ladder climbing on the 1.26 s beat - defaults `bow`=0.16, `draw_s`=0.55, `width`=9
  - +3.07s -> `species:callout` (The scribble callout species) - the third label lands and the picture now carries the number at every crossing - defaults `label_scale`=2.2
- **proof** japan-tariff-trick / build-short @ 9.22s - members at 9.22, 9.77, 10.48, 11.03, 11.74, 12.29 - `content/video_engine/projects/systems-and-blowups/japan-tariff-trick/build-short/japan-short.timeline.json`
- **status** proven - **count** 4 (a grammar) - **source** recipes_r1 R7; docs/research/runs/p56-recipe-seeds/seeds.jsonl seed R7
- **doctrine** E96 -> docs/portable/OPERATOR-RULINGS.md:2797; BACKLOG R26-103 -> docs/content-video-engine/BACKLOG.md:535

### The nine-proof recap

- **id** `recipe:verdict-recap` - **does** Every proof of the argument re-enters as one object: each flies in on its own phrase, is held large while spoken, recedes to a rail spot, and the whole mosaic bursts on the pivot line 25 s later.
- **acts** SETS, QUOTES - **window** 26s
- **members**
  - +0s -> `dock_payload:stack` (The verdict stack) - the recap dock opens over a world that goes on cutting beneath it

  - +25.25s -> `dock_payload:stack` (The verdict stack) - the rail clears on the pivot line: the nine railed cards burst radially and the recap is over
